===== M0007 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.deriveTextBoundsAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D,String,TextAnchor)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 601-675
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method that calculates the anchor offsets for a string.  
     * Normally, the (x, y) coordinate for drawing text is a point on the 
     * baseline at the left of the text string.  If you add these offsets to 
     * (x, y) and draw the string, then the anchor point should coincide with 
     * the (x, y) point.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param anchor  the anchor point.
     *
     * @return  The offsets.
     */
Method code:
601 |     private static float[] deriveTextBoundsAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D g2,
602 |             String text, TextAnchor anchor) {
603 | 
604 |         float[] result = new float[2];
605 |         FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
606 |         Font f = g2.getFont();
607 |         FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(f);
608 |         Rectangle2D bounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(text, g2, fm);
609 |         LineMetrics metrics = f.getLineMetrics(text, frc);
610 |         float ascent = metrics.getAscent();
611 |         float halfAscent = ascent / 2.0f;
612 |         float descent = metrics.getDescent();
613 |         float leading = metrics.getLeading();
614 |         float xAdj = 0.0f;
615 |         float yAdj = 0.0f;
616 | 
617 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
618 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
619 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
620 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
621 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER) {
622 | 
623 |             xAdj = (float) -bounds.getWidth() / 2.0f;
624 | 
625 |         }
626 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT
627 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT
628 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT
629 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT
630 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
631 | 
632 |             xAdj = (float) -bounds.getWidth();
633 | 
634 |         }
635 | 
636 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT
637 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
638 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
639 | 
640 |             yAdj = -descent - leading + (float) bounds.getHeight();
641 | 
642 |         }
643 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_LEFT
644 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER
645 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
646 | 
647 |             yAdj = halfAscent;
648 | 
649 |         }
650 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
651 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
652 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT) {
653 | 
654 |             yAdj = -descent - leading + (float) (bounds.getHeight() / 2.0);
655 | 
656 |         }
657 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_LEFT
658 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
659 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT) {
660 | 
661 |             yAdj = 0.0f;
662 | 
663 |         }
664 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
665 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
666 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
667 | 
668 |             yAdj = -metrics.getDescent() - metrics.getLeading();
669 | 
670 |         }
671 |         result[0] = xAdj;
672 |         result[1] = yAdj;
673 |         return result;
674 | 
675 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextBlock.calculateDimensions(Graphics2D)

Original range: 163-174
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the width and height of the text block.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The width and height.
     */
Method code:
163 |     public Size2D calculateDimensions(Graphics2D g2) {
164 |         double width = 0.0;
165 |         double height = 0.0;
166 |         Iterator iterator = this.lines.iterator();
167 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
168 |             TextLine line = (TextLine) iterator.next();
169 |             Size2D dimension = line.calculateDimensions(g2);
170 |             width = Math.max(width, dimension.getWidth());
171 |             height = height + dimension.getHeight();
172 |         }
173 |         return new Size2D(width, height);
174 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.refreshTicksVertical(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)

Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the positions of the tick labels for the axis, storing the 
     * results in the tick label list (ready for drawing).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the plot should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     * 
     * @return A list of ticks.
     *
     */
Method code:
1242 |     protected List refreshTicksVertical(Graphics2D g2,
1243 |                                         Rectangle2D dataArea,
1244 |                                         RectangleEdge edge) {
1245 | 
1246 |         List result = new java.util.ArrayList();
1247 |         result.clear();
1248 | 
1249 |         Font tickLabelFont = getTickLabelFont();
1250 |         g2.setFont(tickLabelFont);
1251 |         if (isAutoTickUnitSelection()) {
1252 |             selectAutoTickUnit(g2, dataArea, edge);
1253 |         }
1254 | 
1255 |         double size = getTickUnit().getSize();
1256 |         int count = calculateVisibleTickCount();
1257 |         double lowestTickValue = calculateLowestVisibleTickValue();
1258 | 
1259 |         if (count <= ValueAxis.MAXIMUM_TICK_COUNT) {
1260 |             for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
1261 |                 double currentTickValue = lowestTickValue + (i * size);
1262 |                 String tickLabel;
1263 |                 NumberFormat formatter = getNumberFormatOverride();
1264 |                 if (formatter != null) {
1265 |                     tickLabel = formatter.format(currentTickValue);
1266 |                 }
1267 |                 else {
1268 |                     tickLabel = getTickUnit().valueToString(currentTickValue);
1269 |                 }
1270 | 
1271 |                 TextAnchor anchor = null;
1272 |                 TextAnchor rotationAnchor = null;
1273 |                 double angle = 0.0;
1274 |                 if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1275 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) { 
1276 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1277 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1278 |                         angle = -Math.PI / 2.0;
1279 |                     }
1280 |                     else {
1281 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1282 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1283 |                         angle = Math.PI / 2.0;
1284 |                     }
1285 |                 }
1286 |                 else {
1287 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
1288 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1289 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1290 |                     }
1291 |                     else {
1292 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT;
1293 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT;
1294 |                     }
1295 |                 }
1296 | 
1297 |                 Tick tick = new NumberTick(
1298 |                     new Double(currentTickValue), tickLabel, anchor, 
1299 |                     rotationAnchor, angle
1300 |                 );
1301 |                 result.add(tick);
1302 |             }
1303 |         }
1304 |         return result;
1305 | 
1306 |     }

===== M0021 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.BorderArrangement.arrange(BlockContainer,Graphics2D,RectangleConstraint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/BorderArrangement.java
Original range: 128-176
Documentation:
/**
     * Arranges the items in the specified container, subject to the given 
     * constraint.
     * 
     * @param container  the container.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param constraint  the constraint.
     * 
     * @return The block size.
     */
Method code:
128 |     public Size2D arrange(BlockContainer container, 
129 |                           Graphics2D g2, 
130 |                           RectangleConstraint constraint) {
131 |         RectangleConstraint contentConstraint 
132 |             = container.toContentConstraint(constraint);
133 |         Size2D contentSize = null;
134 |         LengthConstraintType w = contentConstraint.getWidthConstraintType();
135 |         LengthConstraintType h = contentConstraint.getHeightConstraintType();
136 |         if (w == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
137 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
138 |                 contentSize = arrangeNN(container, g2);  
139 |             }
140 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
141 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
142 |             }
143 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
144 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
145 |             }
146 |         }
147 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
148 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
149 |                 contentSize = arrangeFN(container, g2, constraint.getWidth());  
150 |             }
151 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
152 |                 contentSize = arrangeFF(container, g2, constraint);  
153 |             }
154 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
155 |                 contentSize = arrangeFR(container, g2, constraint);  
156 |             }
157 |         }
158 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
159 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
160 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
161 |             }
162 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
163 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
164 |             }
165 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
166 |                 contentSize = arrangeRR(
167 |                     container, constraint.getWidthRange(),
168 |                     constraint.getHeightRange(), g2
169 |                 );  
170 |             }
171 |         }
172 |         return new Size2D(
173 |             container.calculateTotalWidth(contentSize.getWidth()),
174 |             container.calculateTotalHeight(contentSize.getHeight())
175 |         );
176 |     }

/**
     * Compares two ellipses and returns <code>true</code> if they are equal or 
     * both <code>null</code>.
     *

===== M0024 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.DateAxis.selectHorizontalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/DateAxis.java
Original range: 1284-1312
Documentation:
/**
     * Selects an appropriate tick size for the axis.  The strategy is to
     * display as many ticks as possible (selected from a collection of 
     * 'standard' tick units) without the labels overlapping.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area defined by the axes.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     */
Method code:
1284 |     protected void selectHorizontalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D g2, 
1285 |                                                 Rectangle2D dataArea, 
1286 |                                                 RectangleEdge edge) {
1287 | 
1288 |         long shift = 0;
1289 |         if (this.timeline instanceof SegmentedTimeline) {
1290 |             shift = ((SegmentedTimeline) this.timeline).getStartTime();
1291 |         }
1292 |         double zero = valueToJava2D(shift + 0.0, dataArea, edge);
1293 |         double tickLabelWidth 
1294 |             = estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(g2, getTickUnit());
1295 | 
1296 |         // start with the current tick unit...
1297 |         TickUnitSource tickUnits = getStandardTickUnits();
1298 |         TickUnit unit1 = tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(getTickUnit());
1299 |         double x1 = valueToJava2D(shift + unit1.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1300 |         double unit1Width = Math.abs(x1 - zero);
1301 | 
1302 |         // then extrapolate...
1303 |         double guess = (tickLabelWidth / unit1Width) * unit1.getSize();
1304 |         DateTickUnit unit2 = (DateTickUnit) tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(guess);
1305 |         double x2 = valueToJava2D(shift + unit2.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1306 |         double unit2Width = Math.abs(x2 - zero);
1307 |         tickLabelWidth = estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(g2, unit2);
1308 |         if (tickLabelWidth > unit2Width) {
1309 |             unit2 = (DateTickUnit) tickUnits.getLargerTickUnit(unit2);
1310 |         }
1311 |         setTickUnit(unit2, false, false);
1312 |     }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.ObjectUtilities.isJDK14()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/ObjectUtilities.java
Original range: 440-471
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns <code>true</code> if we are running on JRE 1.4 or later.
     * 
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
440 |     public static boolean isJDK14() {
441 |         ClassLoader loader = getClassLoader(ObjectUtilities.class);
442 |         if (loader != null) {
443 |             try {
444 |               loader.loadClass("java.util.RandomAccess");
445 |               return true;
446 |             }
447 |             catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
448 |               return false;
449 |             }
450 |             catch(Exception e) {
451 |               // do nothing, but do not crash ...
452 |             }
453 |         }
454 |         // OK, the quick and dirty, but secure way failed. Lets try it
455 |         // using the standard way.
456 |         try {
457 |             String version = System.getProperty(
458 |                     "java.vm.specification.version");
459 |             // parse the beast...
460 |             if (version == null) {
461 |                 return false;
462 |             }
463 | 
464 |             String[] versions = parseVersions(version);
465 |             String[] target = new String[]{ "1", "4" };
466 |             return (ArrayUtilities.compareVersionArrays(versions, target) >= 0);
467 |         }
468 |         catch(Exception e) {
469 |             return false;
470 |         }
471 |     }

Original range: 211-283

/**
     * Draws the background for the plot.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plot  the plot.
     * @param dataArea  the area inside the axes.
     */
Method code:
211 |     public void drawBackground(Graphics2D g2, CategoryPlot plot, 
212 |                                Rectangle2D dataArea) {
213 | 
214 |         float x0 = (float) dataArea.getX();
215 |         float x1 = x0 + (float) Math.abs(this.xOffset);
216 |         float x3 = (float) dataArea.getMaxX();
217 |         float x2 = x3 - (float) Math.abs(this.xOffset);
218 | 
219 |         float y0 = (float) dataArea.getMaxY();
220 |         float y1 = y0 - (float) Math.abs(this.yOffset);
221 |         float y3 = (float) dataArea.getMinY();
222 |         float y2 = y3 + (float) Math.abs(this.yOffset);
223 | 
224 |         GeneralPath clip = new GeneralPath();
225 |         clip.moveTo(x0, y0);
226 |         clip.lineTo(x0, y2);
227 |         clip.lineTo(x1, y3);
228 |         clip.lineTo(x3, y3);
229 |         clip.lineTo(x3, y1);
230 |         clip.lineTo(x2, y0);
231 |         clip.closePath();
232 | 
233 |         Composite originalComposite = g2.getComposite();
234 |         g2.setComposite(AlphaComposite.getInstance(AlphaComposite.SRC_OVER,
235 |                 plot.getBackgroundAlpha()));
236 | 
237 |         // fill background...
238 |         Paint backgroundPaint = plot.getBackgroundPaint();
239 |         if (backgroundPaint != null) {
240 |             g2.setPaint(backgroundPaint);
241 |             g2.fill(clip);
242 |         }
243 | 
244 |         GeneralPath leftWall = new GeneralPath();
245 |         leftWall.moveTo(x0, y0);
246 |         leftWall.lineTo(x0, y2);
247 |         leftWall.lineTo(x1, y3);
248 |         leftWall.lineTo(x1, y1);
249 |         leftWall.closePath();
250 |         g2.setPaint(getWallPaint());
251 |         g2.fill(leftWall);
252 | 
253 |         GeneralPath bottomWall = new GeneralPath();
254 |         bottomWall.moveTo(x0, y0);
255 |         bottomWall.lineTo(x1, y1);
256 |         bottomWall.lineTo(x3, y1);
257 |         bottomWall.lineTo(x2, y0);
258 |         bottomWall.closePath();
259 |         g2.setPaint(getWallPaint());
260 |         g2.fill(bottomWall);
261 | 
262 |         // higlight the background corners...
263 |         g2.setPaint(Color.lightGray);
264 |         Line2D corner = new Line2D.Double(x0, y0, x1, y1);
265 |         g2.draw(corner);
266 |         corner.setLine(x1, y1, x1, y3);
267 |         g2.draw(corner);
268 |         corner.setLine(x1, y1, x3, y1);
269 |         g2.draw(corner);
270 | 
271 |         // draw background image, if there is one...
272 |         Image backgroundImage = plot.getBackgroundImage();
273 |         if (backgroundImage != null) {
274 |             Rectangle2D adjusted = new Rectangle2D.Double(dataArea.getX() 
275 |                     + getXOffset(), dataArea.getY(), 
276 |                     dataArea.getWidth() - getXOffset(), 
277 |                     dataArea.getHeight() - getYOffset());
278 |             plot.drawBackgroundImage(g2, adjusted);
279 |         }
280 |         
281 |         g2.setComposite(originalComposite);
282 | 
283 |     }

/**
     * A utility method for drawing the plot's axes.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plotArea  the plot area.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param plotState  collects information about the plot (<code>null</code>
     *                   permitted).
     * 
     * @return A map containing the axis states.
     */
Method code:
2679 |     protected Map drawAxes(Graphics2D g2, 
2680 |                            Rectangle2D plotArea, 
2681 |                            Rectangle2D dataArea,
2682 |                            PlotRenderingInfo plotState) {
2683 | 
2684 |         AxisCollection axisCollection = new AxisCollection();
2685 | 
2686 |         // add domain axes to lists...
2687 |         for (int index = 0; index < this.domainAxes.size(); index++) {
2688 |             CategoryAxis xAxis = (CategoryAxis) this.domainAxes.get(index);
2689 |             if (xAxis != null) {
2690 |                 axisCollection.add(xAxis, getDomainAxisEdge(index));
2691 |             }
2692 |         }
2693 | 
2694 |         // add range axes to lists...
2695 |         for (int index = 0; index < this.rangeAxes.size(); index++) {
2696 |             ValueAxis yAxis = (ValueAxis) this.rangeAxes.get(index);
2697 |             if (yAxis != null) {
2698 |                 axisCollection.add(yAxis, getRangeAxisEdge(index));
2699 |             }
2700 |         }
2701 | 
2702 |         Map axisStateMap = new HashMap();
2703 |         
2704 |         // draw the top axes
2705 |         double cursor = dataArea.getMinY() - this.axisOffset.calculateTopOutset(
2706 |                 dataArea.getHeight());
2707 |         Iterator iterator = axisCollection.getAxesAtTop().iterator();
2708 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
2709 |             Axis axis = (Axis) iterator.next();
2710 |             if (axis != null) {
2711 |                 AxisState axisState = axis.draw(g2, cursor, plotArea, dataArea, 
2712 |                         RectangleEdge.TOP, plotState);
2713 |                 cursor = axisState.getCursor();
2714 |                 axisStateMap.put(axis, axisState);
2715 |             }
2716 |         }
2717 | 
2718 |         // draw the bottom axes
2719 |         cursor = dataArea.getMaxY() 
2720 |                  + this.axisOffset.calculateBottomOutset(dataArea.getHeight());
2721 |         iterator = axisCollection.getAxesAtBottom().iterator();
2722 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
2723 |             Axis axis = (Axis) iterator.next();
2724 |             if (axis != null) {
2725 |                 AxisState axisState = axis.draw(g2, cursor, plotArea, dataArea,
2726 |                         RectangleEdge.BOTTOM, plotState);
2727 |                 cursor = axisState.getCursor();
2728 |                 axisStateMap.put(axis, axisState);
2729 |             }
2730 |         }
2731 | 
2732 |         // draw the left axes
2733 |         cursor = dataArea.getMinX() 
2734 |                  - this.axisOffset.calculateLeftOutset(dataArea.getWidth());
2735 |         iterator = axisCollection.getAxesAtLeft().iterator();
2736 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
2737 |             Axis axis = (Axis) iterator.next();
2738 |             if (axis != null) {
2739 |                 AxisState axisState = axis.draw(g2, cursor, plotArea, dataArea,
2740 |                         RectangleEdge.LEFT, plotState);
2741 |                 cursor = axisState.getCursor();
2742 |                 axisStateMap.put(axis, axisState);
2743 |             }
2744 |         }
2745 | 
2746 |         // draw the right axes
2747 |         cursor = dataArea.getMaxX() 
2748 |                  + this.axisOffset.calculateRightOutset(dataArea.getWidth());
2749 |         iterator = axisCollection.getAxesAtRight().iterator();
2750 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
2751 |             Axis axis = (Axis) iterator.next();

===== M0044 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.CategoryPlot.calculateRangeAxisSpace(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,AxisSpace)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/CategoryPlot.java
Original range: 2461-2496
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the space required for the range axis/axes.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plotArea  the plot area.
     * @param space  a carrier for the result (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return The required space.
     */
Method code:
2461 |     protected AxisSpace calculateRangeAxisSpace(Graphics2D g2, 
2462 |                                                 Rectangle2D plotArea, 
2463 |                                                 AxisSpace space) {
2464 |                                                   
2465 |         if (space == null) {
2466 |             space = new AxisSpace(); 
2467 |         }
2468 |         
2469 |         // reserve some space for the range axis...
2470 |         if (this.fixedRangeAxisSpace != null) {
2471 |             if (this.orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
2472 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedRangeAxisSpace.getTop(), 
2473 |                         RectangleEdge.TOP);
2474 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedRangeAxisSpace.getBottom(), 
2475 |                         RectangleEdge.BOTTOM);
2476 |             }
2477 |             else if (this.orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
2478 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedRangeAxisSpace.getLeft(), 
2479 |                         RectangleEdge.LEFT);
2480 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedRangeAxisSpace.getRight(), 
2481 |                         RectangleEdge.RIGHT);
2482 |             }
2483 |         }
2484 |         else {
2485 |             // reserve space for the range axes (if any)...
2486 |             for (int i = 0; i < this.rangeAxes.size(); i++) {
2487 |                 Axis yAxis = (Axis) this.rangeAxes.get(i);
2488 |                 if (yAxis != null) {
2489 |                     RectangleEdge edge = getRangeAxisEdge(i);
2490 |                     space = yAxis.reserveSpace(g2, this, plotArea, edge, space);
2491 |                 }
2492 |             }
2493 |         }
2494 |         return space;
2495 |                                                     
2496 |     }

     * <li><code>obj</code> is not <code>null</code>;</li>
     * <li><code>obj</code> is an instance of <code>ValueMarker</code>;</li>
     * <li><code>obj</code> has the same value as this marker;</li>
     * <li><code>super.equals(obj)</code> returns <code>true</code>.</li>
     * </ul>
     * 
     * @param obj  the object (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
136 |     public boolean equals(Object obj) {
137 |         if (obj == this) {
138 |             return true;
139 |         }
140 |         if (!super.equals(obj)) {
141 |             return false;
142 |         }
143 |         if (!(obj instanceof ValueMarker)) {
144 |             return false;
145 |         }
146 |         ValueMarker that = (ValueMarker) obj;
147 |         if (this.value != that.value) {
148 |             return false;
149 |         }
150 |         return true;
151 |     }

/**
     * Arranges the blocks without any constraints.  This puts all blocks
     * into a single row.

     * @param container  the container.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The size after the arrangement.
     */
Method code:
358 |     protected Size2D arrangeNN(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2) {
359 |         double x = 0.0;
360 |         double width = 0.0;
361 |         double maxHeight = 0.0;
362 |         List blocks = container.getBlocks();
363 |         int blockCount = blocks.size();
364 |         if (blockCount > 0) {
365 |             Size2D[] sizes = new Size2D[blocks.size()];
366 |             for (int i = 0; i < blocks.size(); i++) {
367 |                 Block block = (Block) blocks.get(i);
368 |                 sizes[i] = block.arrange(g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE);
369 |                 width = width + sizes[i].getWidth();
370 |                 maxHeight = Math.max(sizes[i].height, maxHeight);
371 |                 block.setBounds(
372 |                     new Rectangle2D.Double(
373 |                         x, 0.0, sizes[i].width, sizes[i].height
374 |                     )
375 |                 );
376 |                 x = x + sizes[i].width + this.horizontalGap;
377 |             }
378 |             if (blockCount > 1) {
379 |                 width = width + this.horizontalGap * (blockCount - 1);   
380 |             }
381 |             if (this.verticalAlignment != VerticalAlignment.TOP) {
382 |                 for (int i = 0; i < blocks.size(); i++) {
383 |                     //Block b = (Block) blocks.get(i);
384 |                     if (this.verticalAlignment == VerticalAlignment.CENTER) {
385 |                         //TODO: shift block down by half
386 |                     }
387 |                     else if (this.verticalAlignment 
388 |                             == VerticalAlignment.BOTTOM) {
389 |                         //TODO: shift block down to bottom
390 |                     }
391 |                 }            
392 |             }
393 |         }
394 |         return new Size2D(width, maxHeight);

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.reserveSpace(Graphics2D,Plot,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge,AxisSpace)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 731-783
Documentation:
/**
     * Estimates the space required for the axis, given a specific drawing area.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (used to obtain font information).
     * @param plot  the plot that the axis belongs to.
     * @param plotArea  the area within which the axis should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location (top or bottom).
     * @param space  the space already reserved.
     *
     * @return The space required to draw the axis.
     */
Method code:
731 |     public AxisSpace reserveSpace(Graphics2D g2, Plot plot, 
732 |                                   Rectangle2D plotArea, 
733 |                                   RectangleEdge edge, AxisSpace space) {
734 | 
735 |         // create a new space object if one wasn't supplied...
736 |         if (space == null) {
737 |             space = new AxisSpace();
738 |         }
739 |         
740 |         // if the axis is not visible, no additional space is required...
741 |         if (!isVisible()) {
742 |             return space;
743 |         }
744 | 
745 |         // calculate the max size of the tick labels (if visible)...
746 |         double tickLabelHeight = 0.0;
747 |         double tickLabelWidth = 0.0;
748 |         if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
749 |             g2.setFont(getTickLabelFont());
750 |             AxisState state = new AxisState();
751 |             // we call refresh ticks just to get the maximum width or height
752 |             refreshTicks(g2, state, plotArea, edge);
753 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
754 |                 tickLabelHeight = state.getMax();
755 |             }
756 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
757 |                 tickLabelHeight = state.getMax();
758 |             }
759 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
760 |                 tickLabelWidth = state.getMax(); 
761 |             }
762 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
763 |                 tickLabelWidth = state.getMax(); 
764 |             }
765 |         }
766 |         
767 |         // get the axis label size and update the space object...
768 |         Rectangle2D labelEnclosure = getLabelEnclosure(g2, edge);
769 |         double labelHeight = 0.0;
770 |         double labelWidth = 0.0;
771 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
772 |             labelHeight = labelEnclosure.getHeight();
773 |             space.add(labelHeight + tickLabelHeight 
774 |                     + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset, edge);
775 |         }
776 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
777 |             labelWidth = labelEnclosure.getWidth();
778 |             space.add(labelWidth + tickLabelWidth 
779 |                     + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset, edge);
780 |         }
781 |         return space;
782 | 
783 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.findMaximumTickLabelHeight(List,Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 812-842
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method for determining the height of the tallest tick label.
     *
     * @param ticks  the ticks.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param drawArea  the area within which the plot and axes should be drawn.
     * @param vertical  a flag that indicates whether or not the tick labels 
     *                  are 'vertical'.
     *
     * @return The height of the tallest tick label.
     */
Method code:
812 |     protected double findMaximumTickLabelHeight(List ticks,
813 |                                                 Graphics2D g2, 
814 |                                                 Rectangle2D drawArea, 
815 |                                                 boolean vertical) {
816 |                                                     
817 |         RectangleInsets insets = getTickLabelInsets();
818 |         Font font = getTickLabelFont();
819 |         double maxHeight = 0.0;
820 |         if (vertical) {
821 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(font);
822 |             Iterator iterator = ticks.iterator();
823 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
824 |                 Tick tick = (Tick) iterator.next();
825 |                 Rectangle2D labelBounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(
826 |                         tick.getText(), g2, fm);
827 |                 if (labelBounds.getWidth() + insets.getTop() 
828 |                         + insets.getBottom() > maxHeight) {
829 |                     maxHeight = labelBounds.getWidth() 
830 |                                 + insets.getTop() + insets.getBottom();
831 |                 }
832 |             }
833 |         }
834 |         else {
835 |             LineMetrics metrics = font.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", 
836 |                     g2.getFontRenderContext());
837 |             maxHeight = metrics.getHeight() 
838 |                         + insets.getTop() + insets.getBottom();
839 |         }
840 |         return maxHeight;
841 |         
842 |     }

Original range: 148-162
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the index for a given key.
     *
     * @param key  the key (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The index, or <code>-1</code> if the key is not recognised.
     * 
     * @throws IllegalArgumentException if <code>key</code> is 
     *     <code>null</code>.
     */
Method code:
148 |     public int getIndex(Comparable key) {
149 |         if (key == null) {
150 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'key' argument.");
151 |         }
152 |         int i = 0;
153 |         Iterator iterator = this.data.iterator();
154 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
155 |             KeyedValue kv = (KeyedValue) iterator.next();
156 |             if (kv.getKey().equals(key)) {
157 |                 return i;
158 |             }
159 |             i++;
160 |         }
161 |         return -1;  // key not found
162 |     }

/**
     * Returns the number of the last day of the month, taking into account 
     * leap years.
     *
     * @param month  the month.
     * @param yyyy  the year (in the range 1900 to 9999).
     *
     * @return the number of the last day of the month.
     */
Method code:
544 |     public static int lastDayOfMonth(final int month, final int yyyy) {
545 | 
546 |         final int result = LAST_DAY_OF_MONTH[month];
547 |         if (month != FEBRUARY) {
548 |             return result;
549 |         }
550 |         else if (isLeapYear(yyyy)) {
551 |             return result + 1;
552 |         }
553 |         else {
554 |             return result;
555 |         }
556 | 
557 |     }

/**
     * Restores a serialized object.
     *
     * @param stream  the input stream.
     *
     * @throws IOException if there is an I/O problem.
     * @throws ClassNotFoundException if there is a problem loading a class.
     */
Method code:
472 |     private void readObject(ObjectInputStream stream) 
473 |         throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {
474 |         stream.defaultReadObject();
475 | 
476 |         int paintCount = stream.readInt();
477 |         this.paintSequence = new Paint[paintCount];
478 |         for (int i = 0; i < paintCount; i++) {
479 |             this.paintSequence[i] = SerialUtilities.readPaint(stream);
480 |         }
481 | 
482 |         int outlinePaintCount = stream.readInt();
483 |         this.outlinePaintSequence = new Paint[outlinePaintCount];
484 |         for (int i = 0; i < outlinePaintCount; i++) {
485 |             this.outlinePaintSequence[i] = SerialUtilities.readPaint(stream);
486 |         }
487 | 
488 |         int strokeCount = stream.readInt();
489 |         this.strokeSequence = new Stroke[strokeCount];
490 |         for (int i = 0; i < strokeCount; i++) {
491 |             this.strokeSequence[i] = SerialUtilities.readStroke(stream);
492 |         }
493 | 
494 |         int outlineStrokeCount = stream.readInt();
495 |         this.outlineStrokeSequence = new Stroke[outlineStrokeCount];
496 |         for (int i = 0; i < outlineStrokeCount; i++) {
497 |             this.outlineStrokeSequence[i] = SerialUtilities.readStroke(stream);
498 |         }
499 | 
500 |         int shapeCount = stream.readInt();
501 |         this.shapeSequence = new Shape[shapeCount];
502 |         for (int i = 0; i < shapeCount; i++) {
503 |             this.shapeSequence[i] = SerialUtilities.readShape(stream);
504 |         }

506 |     }

===== M0099 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.title.TextTitle.arrangeRR(Graphics2D,Range,Range)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/title/TextTitle.java
Original range: 494-531
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the content size for the title.  This will reflect the fact that
     * a text title positioned on the left or right of a chart will be rotated
     * 90 degrees.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param widthRange  the width range.
     * @param heightRange  the height range.
     * 
     * @return The content size.
     */
Method code:
494 |     protected Size2D arrangeRR(Graphics2D g2, Range widthRange, 
495 |             Range heightRange) {
496 |         RectangleEdge position = getPosition();
497 |         if (position == RectangleEdge.TOP || position == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
498 |             float maxWidth = (float) widthRange.getUpperBound();
499 |             g2.setFont(this.font);
500 |             this.content = TextUtilities.createTextBlock(this.text, this.font, 
501 |                     this.paint, maxWidth, new G2TextMeasurer(g2));
502 |             this.content.setLineAlignment(this.textAlignment);
503 |             Size2D contentSize = this.content.calculateDimensions(g2);
504 |             if (this.expandToFitSpace) {
505 |                 return new Size2D(maxWidth, contentSize.getHeight());
506 |             }
507 |             else {
508 |                 return contentSize;
509 |             }
510 |         }
511 |         else if (position == RectangleEdge.LEFT || position 
512 |                 == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
513 |             float maxWidth = (float) heightRange.getUpperBound();
514 |             g2.setFont(this.font);
515 |             this.content = TextUtilities.createTextBlock(this.text, this.font, 
516 |                     this.paint, maxWidth, new G2TextMeasurer(g2));
517 |             this.content.setLineAlignment(this.textAlignment);
518 |             Size2D contentSize = this.content.calculateDimensions(g2);
519 |             
520 |             // transpose the dimensions, because the title is rotated
521 |             if (this.expandToFitSpace) {
522 |                 return new Size2D(contentSize.getHeight(), maxWidth);
523 |             }
524 |             else {
525 |                 return new Size2D(contentSize.height, contentSize.width);
526 |             }
527 |         }
528 |         else {
529 |             throw new RuntimeException("Unrecognised exception.");
530 |         }
531 |     }

/**
     * Shrinks the given rectangle by the amount of these insets.
     *

     */
Method code:
521 |     public void trim(Rectangle2D area) {
522 |         double w = area.getWidth();
523 |         double h = area.getHeight();

527 |         double b = calculateBottomInset(h);
528 |         area.setRect(area.getX() + l, area.getY() + t, w - l - r, h - t - b);    
529 |     }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.calculateCategorySize(int,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 662-685
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the size (width or height, depending on the location of the 
     * axis) of a category.
     *
     * @param categoryCount  the number of categories.
     * @param area  the area within which the categories will be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The category size.
     */
Method code:
662 |     protected double calculateCategorySize(int categoryCount, Rectangle2D area,
663 |                                            RectangleEdge edge) {
664 | 
665 |         double result = 0.0;
666 |         double available = 0.0;
667 | 
668 |         if ((edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) || (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
669 |             available = area.getWidth();
670 |         }
671 |         else if ((edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) 
672 |                 || (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT)) {
673 |             available = area.getHeight();
674 |         }
675 |         if (categoryCount > 1) {
676 |             result = available * (1 - getLowerMargin() - getUpperMargin() 
677 |                      - getCategoryMargin());
678 |             result = result / categoryCount;
679 |         }
680 |         else {
681 |             result = available * (1 - getLowerMargin() - getUpperMargin());
682 |         }
683 |         return result;
684 | 
685 |     }

===== M0108 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.Axis.getLabelEnclosure(Graphics2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/Axis.java
Original range: 1054-1077
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns a rectangle that encloses the axis label.  This is typically 
     * used for layout purposes (it gives the maximum dimensions of the label).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param edge  the edge of the plot area along which the axis is measuring.
     *
     * @return The enclosing rectangle.
     */
Method code:
1054 |     protected Rectangle2D getLabelEnclosure(Graphics2D g2, RectangleEdge edge) {
1055 | 
1056 |         Rectangle2D result = new Rectangle2D.Double();
1057 |         String axisLabel = getLabel();
1058 |         if (axisLabel != null && !axisLabel.equals("")) {
1059 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(getLabelFont());
1060 |             Rectangle2D bounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(axisLabel, g2, fm);
1061 |             RectangleInsets insets = getLabelInsets();
1062 |             bounds = insets.createOutsetRectangle(bounds);
1063 |             double angle = getLabelAngle();
1064 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
1065 |                 angle = angle - Math.PI / 2.0;
1066 |             }
1067 |             double x = bounds.getCenterX();
1068 |             double y = bounds.getCenterY();
1069 |             AffineTransform transformer 
1070 |                 = AffineTransform.getRotateInstance(angle, x, y);
1071 |             Shape labelBounds = transformer.createTransformedShape(bounds);
1072 |             result = labelBounds.getBounds2D();
1073 |         }
1074 | 
1075 |         return result;
1076 | 
1077 |     }

===== M0129 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.createTextBlock(String,Font,Paint,float,int,TextMeasurer)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 185-223
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new text block from the given string, breaking the
     * text into lines so that the <code>maxWidth</code> value is
     * respected.
     * 
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param font  the font.
     * @param paint  the paint.
     * @param maxWidth  the maximum width for each line.
     * @param maxLines  the maximum number of lines.
     * @param measurer  the text measurer.
     * 
     * @return A text block.
     */
Method code:
185 |     public static TextBlock createTextBlock(String text, Font font,
186 |             Paint paint, float maxWidth, int maxLines, TextMeasurer measurer) {
187 |         
188 |         TextBlock result = new TextBlock();
189 |         BreakIterator iterator = BreakIterator.getLineInstance();
190 |         iterator.setText(text);
191 |         int current = 0;
192 |         int lines = 0;
193 |         int length = text.length();
194 |         while (current < length && lines < maxLines) {
195 |             int next = nextLineBreak(text, current, maxWidth, iterator, 
196 |                     measurer);
197 |             if (next == BreakIterator.DONE) {
198 |                 result.addLine(text.substring(current), font, paint);
199 |                 return result;
200 |             }
201 |             result.addLine(text.substring(current, next), font, paint);
202 |             lines++;
203 |             current = next;
204 |             while (current < text.length()&& text.charAt(current) == '\n') {
205 |                 current++;
206 |             }
207 |         }
208 |         if (current < length) {
209 |             TextLine lastLine = result.getLastLine();
210 |             TextFragment lastFragment = lastLine.getLastTextFragment();
211 |             String oldStr = lastFragment.getText();
212 |             String newStr = "...";
213 |             if (oldStr.length() > 3) {
214 |                 newStr = oldStr.substring(0, oldStr.length() - 3) + "...";
215 |             }
216 | 
217 |             lastLine.removeFragment(lastFragment);
218 |             TextFragment newFragment = new TextFragment(newStr, 
219 |                     lastFragment.getFont(), lastFragment.getPaint());
220 |             lastLine.addFragment(newFragment);
221 |         }
222 |         return result;
223 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.CategoryPlot.calculateDomainAxisSpace(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,AxisSpace)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/CategoryPlot.java
Original range: 2406-2450
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the space required for the domain axis/axes.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plotArea  the plot area.
     * @param space  a carrier for the result (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return The required space.
     */
Method code:
2406 |     protected AxisSpace calculateDomainAxisSpace(Graphics2D g2, 
2407 |                                                  Rectangle2D plotArea, 
2408 |                                                  AxisSpace space) {
2409 |                                                      
2410 |         if (space == null) {
2411 |             space = new AxisSpace();
2412 |         }
2413 |         
2414 |         // reserve some space for the domain axis...
2415 |         if (this.fixedDomainAxisSpace != null) {
2416 |             if (this.orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
2417 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(
2418 |                     this.fixedDomainAxisSpace.getLeft(), RectangleEdge.LEFT);
2419 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedDomainAxisSpace.getRight(), 
2420 |                         RectangleEdge.RIGHT);
2421 |             }
2422 |             else if (this.orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
2423 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedDomainAxisSpace.getTop(), 
2424 |                         RectangleEdge.TOP);
2425 |                 space.ensureAtLeast(this.fixedDomainAxisSpace.getBottom(), 
2426 |                         RectangleEdge.BOTTOM);
2427 |             }
2428 |         }
2429 |         else {
2430 |             // reserve space for the primary domain axis...
2431 |             RectangleEdge domainEdge = Plot.resolveDomainAxisLocation(
2432 |                     getDomainAxisLocation(), this.orientation);
2433 |             if (this.drawSharedDomainAxis) {
2434 |                 space = getDomainAxis().reserveSpace(g2, this, plotArea, 
2435 |                         domainEdge, space);
2436 |             }
2437 |             
2438 |             // reserve space for any domain axes...
2439 |             for (int i = 0; i < this.domainAxes.size(); i++) {
2440 |                 Axis xAxis = (Axis) this.domainAxes.get(i);
2441 |                 if (xAxis != null) {
2442 |                     RectangleEdge edge = getDomainAxisEdge(i);
2443 |                     space = xAxis.reserveSpace(g2, this, plotArea, edge, space);
2444 |                 }
2445 |             }
2446 |         }
2447 | 
2448 |         return space;
2449 |                                                      
2450 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextBlock.calculateOffsets(TextBlockAnchor,double,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextBlock.java
Original range: 266-311
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the x and y offsets required to align the text block with the
     * specified anchor point.  This assumes that the top left of the text 
     * block is at (0.0, 0.0).
     * 
     * @param anchor  the anchor position.
     * @param width  the width of the text block.
     * @param height  the height of the text block.
     * 
     * @return The offsets (float[0] = x offset, float[1] = y offset).
     */
Method code:
266 |     private float[] calculateOffsets(TextBlockAnchor anchor, 
267 |                                      double width, double height) {
268 |         float[] result = new float[2];
269 |         float xAdj = 0.0f;
270 |         float yAdj = 0.0f;
271 | 
272 |         if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_CENTER
273 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER
274 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER) {
275 |                     
276 |             xAdj = (float) -width / 2.0f;
277 |             
278 |         }
279 |         else if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_RIGHT
280 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT
281 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
282 |                     
283 |             xAdj = (float) -width;
284 |             
285 |         }
286 | 
287 |         if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_LEFT
288 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_CENTER
289 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
290 |                     
291 |             yAdj = 0.0f;
292 |             
293 |         }
294 |         else if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
295 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER
296 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT) {
297 |                     
298 |             yAdj = (float) -height / 2.0f;
299 |             
300 |         }
301 |         else if (anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
302 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
303 |                 || anchor == TextBlockAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
304 |                     
305 |             yAdj = (float) -height;
306 |             
307 |         }
308 |         result[0] = xAdj;
309 |         result[1] = yAdj;
310 |         return result;
311 |     }

     * range axis, and an {@link AreaRenderer} as the renderer.

     *                     permitted).
     * @param legend  a flag specifying whether or not a legend is required.
     * @param tooltips  configure chart to generate tool tips?
     * @param urls  configure chart to generate URLs?
     *
     * @return An area chart.
     */
Method code:

835 |                                              String categoryAxisLabel,

===== M0176 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.SerialUtilities.writeStroke(Stroke,ObjectOutputStream)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/SerialUtilities.java
Original range: 222-248
Documentation:
/**
     * Serialises a <code>Stroke</code> object.  This code handles the
     * <code>BasicStroke</code> class which is the only <code>Stroke</code> 
     * implementation provided by the JDK (and isn't directly 
     * <code>Serializable</code>).
     *
     * @param stroke  the stroke object (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param stream  the output stream (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @throws IOException if there is an I/O error.
     */
Method code:
222 |     public static void writeStroke(Stroke stroke, ObjectOutputStream stream) 
223 |             throws IOException {
224 | 
225 |         if (stream == null) {
226 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'stream' argument.");   
227 |         }
228 |         if (stroke != null) {
229 |             stream.writeBoolean(false);
230 |             if (stroke instanceof BasicStroke) {
231 |                 BasicStroke s = (BasicStroke) stroke;
232 |                 stream.writeObject(BasicStroke.class);
233 |                 stream.writeFloat(s.getLineWidth());
234 |                 stream.writeInt(s.getEndCap());
235 |                 stream.writeInt(s.getLineJoin());
236 |                 stream.writeFloat(s.getMiterLimit());
237 |                 stream.writeObject(s.getDashArray());
238 |                 stream.writeFloat(s.getDashPhase());
239 |             }
240 |             else {
241 |                 stream.writeObject(stroke.getClass());
242 |                 stream.writeObject(stroke);
243 |             }
244 |         }
245 |         else {
246 |             stream.writeBoolean(true);
247 |         }
248 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the width and height of the text line.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The width and height.
     */
Method code:
178 |     public Size2D calculateDimensions(Graphics2D g2) {
179 |         double width = 0.0;
180 |         double height = 0.0;
181 |         Iterator iterator = this.fragments.iterator();
182 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
183 |             TextFragment fragment = (TextFragment) iterator.next();
184 |             Size2D dimension = fragment.calculateDimensions(g2);
185 |             width = width + dimension.getWidth();
186 |             height = Math.max(height, dimension.getHeight());
187 |         }
188 |         return new Size2D(width, height);
189 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.calculateCategoryGapSize(int,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 697-718
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the size (width or height, depending on the location of the 
     * axis) of a category gap.
     *
     * @param categoryCount  the number of categories.
     * @param area  the area within which the categories will be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The category gap width.
     */
Method code:
697 |     protected double calculateCategoryGapSize(int categoryCount, 
698 |                                               Rectangle2D area,
699 |                                               RectangleEdge edge) {
700 | 
701 |         double result = 0.0;
702 |         double available = 0.0;
703 | 
704 |         if ((edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) || (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
705 |             available = area.getWidth();
706 |         }
707 |         else if ((edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) 
708 |                 || (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT)) {
709 |             available = area.getHeight();
710 |         }
711 | 
712 |         if (categoryCount > 1) {
713 |             result = available * getCategoryMargin() / (categoryCount - 1);
714 |         }
715 | 
716 |         return result;
717 | 
718 |     }

/**
     * Draws the text block, aligning it with the specified anchor point and 
     * rotating it about the specified rotation point.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param anchorX  the x-coordinate for the anchor point.
     * @param anchorY  the y-coordinate for the anchor point.
     * @param anchor  the point on the text block that is aligned to the 
     *                anchor point.
     * @param rotateX  the x-coordinate for the rotation point.
     * @param rotateY  the x-coordinate for the rotation point.
     * @param angle  the rotation (in radians).
     */
Method code:
228 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, float anchorX, float anchorY, 
229 |                      TextBlockAnchor anchor, float rotateX, float rotateY, 
230 |                      double angle) {
231 |     
232 |         Size2D d = calculateDimensions(g2);
233 |         float[] offsets = calculateOffsets(anchor, d.getWidth(), d.getHeight());
234 |         Iterator iterator = this.lines.iterator();
235 |         float yCursor = 0.0f;
236 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
237 |             TextLine line = (TextLine) iterator.next();
238 |             Size2D dimension = line.calculateDimensions(g2);
239 |             float lineOffset = 0.0f;
240 |             if (this.lineAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.CENTER) {
241 |                 lineOffset = (float) (d.getWidth() - dimension.getWidth()) 
242 |                     / 2.0f;   
243 |             }
244 |             else if (this.lineAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.RIGHT) {
245 |                 lineOffset = (float) (d.getWidth() - dimension.getWidth());   
246 |             }
247 |             line.draw(g2, anchorX + offsets[0] + lineOffset, anchorY 
248 |                     + offsets[1] + yCursor, TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT, rotateX, 
249 |                     rotateY, angle);
250 |             yCursor = yCursor + (float) dimension.getHeight();
251 |         }
252 |         
253 |     }

===== M0220 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.findMaximumTickLabelWidth(List,Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 855-885
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method for determining the width of the widest tick label.
     *
     * @param ticks  the ticks.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param drawArea  the area within which the plot and axes should be drawn.
     * @param vertical  a flag that indicates whether or not the tick labels 
     *                  are 'vertical'.
     *
     * @return The width of the tallest tick label.
     */
Method code:
855 |     protected double findMaximumTickLabelWidth(List ticks, 
856 |                                                Graphics2D g2, 
857 |                                                Rectangle2D drawArea, 
858 |                                                boolean vertical) {
859 |                                                    
860 |         RectangleInsets insets = getTickLabelInsets();
861 |         Font font = getTickLabelFont();
862 |         double maxWidth = 0.0;
863 |         if (!vertical) {
864 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(font);
865 |             Iterator iterator = ticks.iterator();
866 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
867 |                 Tick tick = (Tick) iterator.next();
868 |                 Rectangle2D labelBounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(
869 |                         tick.getText(), g2, fm);
870 |                 if (labelBounds.getWidth() + insets.getLeft() 
871 |                         + insets.getRight() > maxWidth) {
872 |                     maxWidth = labelBounds.getWidth() 
873 |                                + insets.getLeft() + insets.getRight();
874 |                 }
875 |             }
876 |         }
877 |         else {
878 |             LineMetrics metrics = font.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", 
879 |                     g2.getFontRenderContext());
880 |             maxWidth = metrics.getHeight() 
881 |                        + insets.getTop() + insets.getBottom();
882 |         }
883 |         return maxWidth;
884 |         
885 |     }

/**
     * Returns the previous "standard" date, for a given date and tick unit.
     *
     * @param date  the reference date.
     * @param unit  the tick unit.
     *
     * @return The previous "standard" date.
     */
Method code:
828 |     protected Date previousStandardDate(Date date, DateTickUnit unit) {
829 | 
830 |         int milliseconds;
831 |         int seconds;
832 |         int minutes;
833 |         int hours;
834 |         int days;
835 |         int months;
836 |         int years;
837 | 
838 |         Calendar calendar = Calendar.getInstance(this.timeZone);
839 |         calendar.setTime(date);
840 |         int count = unit.getCount();
841 |         int current = calendar.get(unit.getCalendarField());
842 |         int value = count * (current / count);
843 | 
844 |         switch (unit.getUnit()) {
845 | 
846 |             case (DateTickUnit.MILLISECOND) :
847 |                 years = calendar.get(Calendar.YEAR);
848 |                 months = calendar.get(Calendar.MONTH);
849 |                 days = calendar.get(Calendar.DATE);

851 |                 minutes = calendar.get(Calendar.MINUTE);

854 |                 calendar.set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, value);
855 |                 Date mm = calendar.getTime();
856 |                 if (mm.getTime() >= date.getTime()) {
857 |                     calendar.set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, value - 1);

860 |                 return calendar.getTime();

867 |                 minutes = calendar.get(Calendar.MINUTE);
868 |                 if (this.tickMarkPosition == DateTickMarkPosition.START) {
869 |                     milliseconds = 0;
870 |                 }
871 |                 else if (this.tickMarkPosition == DateTickMarkPosition.MIDDLE) {
872 |                     milliseconds = 500;
873 |                 }
874 |                 else {
875 |                     milliseconds = 999;
876 |                 }

878 |                 calendar.set(years, months, days, hours, minutes, value);
879 |                 Date dd = calendar.getTime();
880 |                 if (dd.getTime() >= date.getTime()) {
881 |                     calendar.set(Calendar.SECOND, value - 1);
882 |                     dd = calendar.getTime();
883 |                 }
884 |                 return calendar.getTime();
885 |

891 |                 if (this.tickMarkPosition == DateTickMarkPosition.START) {
892 |                     seconds = 0;

895 |                     seconds = 30;

473 |             }
474 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
475 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not yet implemented.");
476 |             }
477 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
478 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not yet implemented.");
479 |             }
480 |         }
481 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
482 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
483 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not yet implemented.");
484 |             }
485 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
486 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not yet implemented.");
487 |             }
488 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {   
489 |                 contentSize = new Size2D(
490 |                     contentConstraint.getWidth(),
491 |                     contentConstraint.getHeight()
492 |                 );
493 |             }            
494 |         }
495 |         return new Size2D(
496 |             calculateTotalWidth(contentSize.getWidth()), 
497 |             calculateTotalHeight(contentSize.getHeight())
498 |         );
499 |     }

/**
     * Reads a <code>Shape</code> object that has been serialised by the 
     * {@link #writeShape(Shape, ObjectOutputStream)} method.
     *
     * @param stream  the input stream (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The shape object (possibly <code>null</code>).
     *
     * @throws IOException  if there is an I/O problem.
     * @throws ClassNotFoundException  if there is a problem loading a class.
     */
Method code:
261 |     public static Shape readShape(ObjectInputStream stream)
262 |             throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {
263 | 
264 |         if (stream == null) {
265 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'stream' argument.");   
266 |         }
267 |         Shape result = null;
268 |         boolean isNull = stream.readBoolean();
269 |         if (!isNull) {
270 |             Class c = (Class) stream.readObject();
271 |             if (c.equals(Line2D.class)) {
272 |                 double x1 = stream.readDouble();
273 |                 double y1 = stream.readDouble();
274 |                 double x2 = stream.readDouble();
275 |                 double y2 = stream.readDouble();
276 |                 result = new Line2D.Double(x1, y1, x2, y2);
277 |             }
278 |             else if (c.equals(Rectangle2D.class)) {
279 |                 double x = stream.readDouble();
280 |                 double y = stream.readDouble();
281 |                 double w = stream.readDouble();
282 |                 double h = stream.readDouble();
283 |                 result = new Rectangle2D.Double(x, y, w, h);
284 |             }
285 |             else if (c.equals(Ellipse2D.class)) {
286 |                 double x = stream.readDouble();
287 |                 double y = stream.readDouble();
288 |                 double w = stream.readDouble();
289 |                 double h = stream.readDouble();
290 |                 result = new Ellipse2D.Double(x, y, w, h);
291 |             }
292 |             else if (c.equals(Arc2D.class)) {
293 |                 double x = stream.readDouble();
294 |                 double y = stream.readDouble();
295 |                 double w = stream.readDouble();
296 |                 double h = stream.readDouble();
297 |                 double as = stream.readDouble(); // Angle Start
298 |                 double ae = stream.readDouble(); // Angle Extent
299 |                 int at = stream.readInt();       // Arc type
300 |                 result = new Arc2D.Double(x, y, w, h, as, ae, at);
301 |             }            
302 |             else if (c.equals(GeneralPath.class)) {
303 |                 GeneralPath gp = new GeneralPath();
304 |                 float[] args = new float[6];
305 |                 boolean hasNext = stream.readBoolean();
306 |                 while (!hasNext) {
307 |                     int type = stream.readInt();
308 |                     for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
309 |                         args[i] = stream.readFloat();
310 |                     }
311 |                     switch (type) { 
312 |                         case PathIterator.SEG_MOVETO :  
313 |                             gp.moveTo(args[0], args[1]);
314 |                             break;
315 |                         case PathIterator.SEG_LINETO :                           
316 |                             gp.lineTo(args[0], args[1]);
317 |                             break; 
318 |                         case PathIterator.SEG_CUBICTO :
319 |                             gp.curveTo(
320 |                                 args[0], args[1], args[2], 
321 |                                 args[3], args[4], args[5]
322 |                             );
323 |                             break;
324 |                         case PathIterator.SEG_QUADTO :
325 |                             gp.quadTo(args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3]);
326 |                             break;                  
327 |                         case PathIterator.SEG_CLOSE :
328 |                             //result = gp;
329 |                             break;
330 |                         default : 
331 |                             throw new RuntimeException(
332 |                                 "JFreeChart - No path exists"
333 |                             ); 
334 |                     } 
335 |                     gp.setWindingRule(stream.readInt());    
336 |                     hasNext = stream.readBoolean();
337 |                 }
338 |                 result = gp;
339 |             }
340 |             else {
341 |                 result = (Shape) stream.readObject();
342 |             }
343 |         }
344 |         return result;
345 | 
346 |     }

/**
     * Fills the specified area with the background paint.  If the background
     * paint is an instance of <code>GradientPaint</code>, the gradient will
     * run in the direction suggested by the plot's orientation.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics target.
     * @param area  the plot area.
     * @param orientation  the plot orientation (<code>null</code> not 
     *         permitted).
     * 
     * @since 1.0.6
     */
Method code:
962 |     protected void fillBackground(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area, 
963 |             PlotOrientation orientation) {

966 |         }
967 |         if (this.backgroundPaint == null) {
968 |             return;
969 |         }

978 |             }

980 |                 p = new GradientPaint((float) area.getMinX(),

988 |                 this.backgroundAlpha));

990 |         g2.fill(area);

/**
     * Draws the chart on a Java 2D graphics device (such as the screen or a
     * printer).
     * <P>
     * This method is the focus of the entire JFreeChart library.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param chartArea  the area within which the chart should be drawn.

     *                (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param info  records info about the drawing (null means collect no info).
     */
Method code:
1126 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, 
1127 |                      Rectangle2D chartArea, Point2D anchor, 
1128 |                      ChartRenderingInfo info) {
1129 |

1131 |                 ChartProgressEvent.DRAWING_STARTED, 0));
1132 |

1156 |                     this.backgroundImage.getWidth(null),

1160 |                     (int) dest.getY(), (int) dest.getWidth(), 
1161 |                     (int) dest.getHeight(), null);

1163 |         }

1167 |             Stroke stroke = getBorderStroke();

1190 |                     (entities != null));

===== M0256 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.DateTickUnit.getMillisecondCount(int,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/DateTickUnit.java
Original range: 350-377
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the (approximate) number of milliseconds for the given unit and 
     * unit count.
     * <P>
     * This value is an approximation some of the time (e.g. months are 
     * assumed to have 31 days) but this shouldn't matter.
     *
     * @param unit  the unit.
     * @param count  the unit count.
     *
     * @return The number of milliseconds.
     */
Method code:
350 |     private static long getMillisecondCount(int unit, int count) {
351 | 
352 |         switch (unit) {
353 |             case (YEAR):
354 |                 return (365L * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
355 |             case (MONTH):
356 |                 return (31L * 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
357 |             case (DAY):
358 |                 return (24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
359 |             case (HOUR):
360 |                 return (60L * 60L * 1000L) * count;
361 |             case (MINUTE):
362 |                 return (60L * 1000L) * count;
363 |             case (SECOND):
364 |                 return 1000L * count;
365 |             case (MILLISECOND):
366 |                 return count;
367 |             default:
368 |                 throw new IllegalArgumentException(
369 |                     "DateTickUnit.getMillisecondCount() : unit must "
370 |                     + "be one of the constants YEAR, MONTH, DAY, HOUR, MINUTE, "
371 |                     + "SECOND or MILLISECOND defined in the DateTickUnit "
372 |                     + "class. Do *not* use the constants defined in "
373 |                     + "java.util.Calendar."
374 |                 );
375 |         }
376 | 
377 |     }

===== M0260 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.reserveSpace(Graphics2D,Plot,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge,AxisSpace)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 748-799
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the space required to draw the axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param plot  the plot that the axis belongs to.
     * @param plotArea  the area within which the plot should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     * @param space  the space already reserved (for other axes).
     *
     * @return The space required to draw the axis (including pre-reserved 
     *         space).
     */
Method code:
748 |     public AxisSpace reserveSpace(Graphics2D g2, Plot plot,
749 |                                   Rectangle2D plotArea, 
750 |                                   RectangleEdge edge, AxisSpace space) {
751 | 
752 |         // create a new space object if one wasn't supplied...
753 |         if (space == null) {
754 |             space = new AxisSpace();
755 |         }
756 |         
757 |         // if the axis is not visible, no additional space is required...
758 |         if (!isVisible()) {
759 |             return space;
760 |         }
761 | 
762 |         // if the axis has a fixed dimension, return it...
763 |         double dimension = getFixedDimension();
764 |         if (dimension > 0.0) {
765 |             space.ensureAtLeast(dimension, edge);
766 |         }
767 | 
768 |         // calculate the max size of the tick labels (if visible)...
769 |         double tickLabelHeight = 0.0;
770 |         double tickLabelWidth = 0.0;
771 |         if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
772 |             g2.setFont(getTickLabelFont());
773 |             List ticks = refreshTicks(g2, new AxisState(), plotArea, edge);
774 |             if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
775 |                 tickLabelHeight = findMaximumTickLabelHeight(ticks, g2, 
776 |                         plotArea, isVerticalTickLabels());
777 |             }
778 |             else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
779 |                 tickLabelWidth = findMaximumTickLabelWidth(ticks, g2, plotArea,
780 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
781 |             }
782 |         }
783 | 
784 |         // get the axis label size and update the space object...
785 |         Rectangle2D labelEnclosure = getLabelEnclosure(g2, edge);
786 |         double labelHeight = 0.0;
787 |         double labelWidth = 0.0;
788 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
789 |             labelHeight = labelEnclosure.getHeight();
790 |             space.add(labelHeight + tickLabelHeight, edge);
791 |         }
792 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
793 |             labelWidth = labelEnclosure.getWidth();
794 |             space.add(labelWidth + tickLabelWidth, edge);
795 |         }
796 | 
797 |         return space;
798 | 
799 |     }

/**
     * Converts a data value to a coordinate in Java2D space, assuming that the
     * axis runs along one edge of the specified dataArea.
     * <p>
     * Note that it is possible for the coordinate to fall outside the plotArea.
     *
     * @param value  the data value.
     * @param area  the area for plotting the data.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The Java2D coordinate.
     * 
     * @see #java2DToValue(double, Rectangle2D, RectangleEdge)
     */
Method code:
522 |     public double valueToJava2D(double value, Rectangle2D area, 
523 |                                 RectangleEdge edge) {
524 |         
525 |         Range range = getRange();
526 |         double axisMin = range.getLowerBound();
527 |         double axisMax = range.getUpperBound();
528 | 
529 |         double min = 0.0;
530 |         double max = 0.0;
531 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
532 |             min = area.getX();
533 |             max = area.getMaxX();
534 |         }
535 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
536 |             max = area.getMinY();
537 |             min = area.getMaxY();
538 |         }
539 |         if (isInverted()) {
540 |             return max 
541 |                    - ((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) * (max - min);
542 |         }
543 |         else {
544 |             return min 
545 |                    + ((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) * (max - min);
546 |         }
547 | 
548 |     }

Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 1248-1265
Documentation:
/**
     * Writes a map of (<code>Comparable</code>, <code>Paint</code>)
     * elements to a stream.
     * 
     * @param map  the map (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @param out
     * @throws IOException
     * 
     * @see #readPaintMap(ObjectInputStream)
     */
Method code:
1248 |     private void writePaintMap(Map map, ObjectOutputStream out) 
1249 |             throws IOException {
1250 |         if (map == null) {
1251 |             out.writeBoolean(true);
1252 |         }
1253 |         else {
1254 |             out.writeBoolean(false);
1255 |             Set keys = map.keySet();
1256 |             int count = keys.size();
1257 |             out.writeInt(count);
1258 |             Iterator iterator = keys.iterator();
1259 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1260 |                 Comparable key = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1261 |                 out.writeObject(key);
1262 |                 SerialUtilities.writePaint((Paint) map.get(key), out);
1263 |             }
1264 |         }
1265 |     }

===== M0301 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.ObjectUtilities.getClassLoader(Class)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/ObjectUtilities.java
Original range: 256-276
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the classloader, which was responsible for loading the given
     * class.
     *
     * @param c the classloader, either an application class loader or the
     *          boot loader.
     * @return the classloader, never null.
     * @throws SecurityException if the SecurityManager does not allow to grab
     *                           the context classloader.
     */
Method code:
256 |     public synchronized static ClassLoader getClassLoader(final Class c) {
257 |         if (classLoader != null) {
258 |             return classLoader;
259 |         }
260 |         if ("ThreadContext".equals(classLoaderSource)) {
261 |             final ClassLoader threadLoader
262 |                 = Thread.currentThread().getContextClassLoader();
263 |             if (threadLoader != null) {
264 |                 return threadLoader;
265 |             }
266 |         }
267 | 
268 |         // Context classloader - do not cache ..
269 |         final ClassLoader applicationCL = c.getClassLoader();
270 |         if (applicationCL == null) {
271 |             return ClassLoader.getSystemClassLoader();
272 |         }
273 |         else {
274 |             return applicationCL;
275 |         }
276 |     }
