===== M0315 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.Plot.resolveDomainAxisLocation(AxisLocation,PlotOrientation)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/Plot.java
Original range: 1304-1354
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolves a domain axis location for a given plot orientation.
     *
     * @param location  the location (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param orientation  the orientation (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The edge (never <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
1304 |     public static RectangleEdge resolveDomainAxisLocation(
1305 |             AxisLocation location, PlotOrientation orientation) {
1306 |         
1307 |         if (location == null) {
1308 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'location' argument.");   
1309 |         }
1310 |         if (orientation == null) {
1311 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
1312 |         }
1313 | 
1314 |         RectangleEdge result = null;
1315 |         
1316 |         if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_RIGHT) {
1317 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1318 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1319 |             }
1320 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1321 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1322 |             }
1323 |         }
1324 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_LEFT) {
1325 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1326 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1327 |             }
1328 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1329 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1330 |             }
1331 |         }
1332 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_RIGHT) {
1333 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1334 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1335 |             }
1336 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1337 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1338 |             }
1339 |         }
1340 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_LEFT) {
1341 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1342 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1343 |             }
1344 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1345 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1346 |             }
1347 |         }
1348 |         // the above should cover all the options...
1349 |         if (result == null) {
1350 |             throw new IllegalStateException("resolveDomainAxisLocation()");
1351 |         }
1352 |         return result;
1353 |         
1354 |     }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.deriveRotationAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D,String,TextAnchor)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 688-770
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method that calculates the rotation anchor offsets for a 
     * string.  These offsets are relative to the text starting coordinate 
     * (BASELINE_LEFT).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param anchor  the anchor point.
     *
     * @return  The offsets.
     */
Method code:
688 |     private static float[] deriveRotationAnchorOffsets(Graphics2D g2,
689 |             String text, TextAnchor anchor) {
690 | 
691 |         float[] result = new float[2];
692 |         FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
693 |         LineMetrics metrics = g2.getFont().getLineMetrics(text, frc);
694 |         FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics();
695 |         Rectangle2D bounds = TextUtilities.getTextBounds(text, g2, fm);
696 |         float ascent = metrics.getAscent();
697 |         float halfAscent = ascent / 2.0f;
698 |         float descent = metrics.getDescent();
699 |         float leading = metrics.getLeading();
700 |         float xAdj = 0.0f;
701 |         float yAdj = 0.0f;
702 | 
703 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT
704 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
705 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
706 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_LEFT
707 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_LEFT) {
708 | 
709 |             xAdj = 0.0f;
710 | 
711 |         }
712 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
713 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
714 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
715 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
716 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER) {
717 | 
718 |             xAdj = (float) bounds.getWidth() / 2.0f;
719 | 
720 |         }
721 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT
722 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT
723 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT
724 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT
725 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
726 | 
727 |             xAdj = (float) bounds.getWidth();
728 | 
729 |         }
730 | 
731 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT
732 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER
733 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_RIGHT) {
734 | 
735 |             yAdj = descent + leading - (float) bounds.getHeight();
736 | 
737 |         }
738 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT
739 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER
740 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT) {
741 | 
742 |             yAdj = descent + leading - (float) (bounds.getHeight() / 2.0);
743 | 
744 |         }
745 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_LEFT
746 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_CENTER
747 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.HALF_ASCENT_RIGHT) {
748 | 
749 |             yAdj = -halfAscent;
750 | 
751 |         }
752 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_LEFT
753 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_CENTER
754 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BASELINE_RIGHT) {
755 | 
756 |             yAdj = 0.0f;
757 | 
758 |         }
759 |         else if (anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_LEFT
760 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER
761 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
762 | 
763 |             yAdj = metrics.getDescent() + metrics.getLeading();
764 | 
765 |         }
766 |         result[0] = xAdj;
767 |         result[1] = yAdj;
768 |         return result;
769 | 
770 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.DateAxis.refreshTicksHorizontal(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/DateAxis.java
Original range: 1502-1590
Documentation:
/**
     * Recalculates the ticks for the date axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the data is to be drawn.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     *
     * @return A list of ticks.
     */
Method code:
1502 |     protected List refreshTicksHorizontal(Graphics2D g2,
1503 |                                           Rectangle2D dataArea,
1504 |                                           RectangleEdge edge) {
1505 | 
1506 |         List result = new java.util.ArrayList();
1507 | 
1508 |         Font tickLabelFont = getTickLabelFont();
1509 |         g2.setFont(tickLabelFont);
1510 | 
1511 |         if (isAutoTickUnitSelection()) {
1512 |             selectAutoTickUnit(g2, dataArea, edge);
1513 |         }
1514 | 
1515 |         DateTickUnit unit = getTickUnit();
1516 |         Date tickDate = calculateLowestVisibleTickValue(unit);
1517 |         Date upperDate = getMaximumDate();
1518 | 
1519 |         while (tickDate.before(upperDate)) {
1520 | 
1521 |             if (!isHiddenValue(tickDate.getTime())) {
1522 |                 // work out the value, label and position
1523 |                 String tickLabel;
1524 |                 DateFormat formatter = getDateFormatOverride();
1525 |                 if (formatter != null) {
1526 |                     tickLabel = formatter.format(tickDate);
1527 |                 }
1528 |                 else {
1529 |                     tickLabel = this.tickUnit.dateToString(tickDate);
1530 |                 }
1531 |                 TextAnchor anchor = null;
1532 |                 TextAnchor rotationAnchor = null;
1533 |                 double angle = 0.0;
1534 |                 if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1535 |                     anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1536 |                     rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1537 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1538 |                         angle = Math.PI / 2.0;
1539 |                     }
1540 |                     else {
1541 |                         angle = -Math.PI / 2.0;
1542 |                     }
1543 |                 }
1544 |                 else {
1545 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1546 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1547 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1548 |                     }
1549 |                     else {
1550 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER;
1551 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER;
1552 |                     }
1553 |                 }
1554 | 
1555 |                 Tick tick = new DateTick(tickDate, tickLabel, anchor, 
1556 |                         rotationAnchor, angle);
1557 |                 result.add(tick);
1558 |                 tickDate = unit.addToDate(tickDate, this.timeZone);
1559 |             }
1560 |             else {
1561 |                 tickDate = unit.rollDate(tickDate, this.timeZone);
1562 |                 continue;
1563 |             }
1564 | 
1565 |             // could add a flag to make the following correction optional...
1566 |             switch (unit.getUnit()) {
1567 | 
1568 |                 case (DateTickUnit.MILLISECOND) :
1569 |                 case (DateTickUnit.SECOND) :
1570 |                 case (DateTickUnit.MINUTE) :
1571 |                 case (DateTickUnit.HOUR) :
1572 |                 case (DateTickUnit.DAY) :
1573 |                     break;
1574 |                 case (DateTickUnit.MONTH) :
1575 |                     tickDate = calculateDateForPosition(new Month(tickDate,
1576 |                             this.timeZone), this.tickMarkPosition);
1577 |                     break;
1578 |                 case(DateTickUnit.YEAR) :
1579 |                     tickDate = calculateDateForPosition(new Year(tickDate, 
1580 |                             this.timeZone), this.tickMarkPosition);
1581 |                     break;
1582 | 
1583 |                 default: break;
1584 | 
1585 |             }
1586 | 
1587 |         }
1588 |         return result;
1589 | 
1590 |     }

     * tick unit is used.
     * <P>
     * Rather than computing the string bounds of every tick on the axis, we
     * just look at two values: the lower bound and the upper bound for the 
     * axis.  These two values will usually be representative.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param unit  the tick unit to use for calculation.
     *
     * @return The estimated maximum width of the tick labels.
     */
Method code:
1373 |     private double estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(Graphics2D g2, 
1374 |                                                  DateTickUnit unit) {
1375 | 
1376 |         RectangleInsets tickLabelInsets = getTickLabelInsets();
1377 |         double result = tickLabelInsets.getLeft() + tickLabelInsets.getRight();
1378 | 
1379 |         Font tickLabelFont = getTickLabelFont();
1380 |         FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
1381 |         LineMetrics lm = tickLabelFont.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", frc);
1382 |         if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1383 |             // all tick labels have the same width (equal to the height of 
1384 |             // the font)...
1385 |             result += lm.getHeight();
1386 |         }
1387 |         else {
1388 |             // look at lower and upper bounds...
1389 |             DateRange range = (DateRange) getRange();
1390 |             Date lower = range.getLowerDate();
1391 |             Date upper = range.getUpperDate();
1392 |             String lowerStr = null;
1393 |             String upperStr = null;
1394 |             DateFormat formatter = getDateFormatOverride();
1395 |             if (formatter != null) {
1396 |                 lowerStr = formatter.format(lower);
1397 |                 upperStr = formatter.format(upper);
1398 |             }
1399 |             else {
1400 |                 lowerStr = unit.dateToString(lower);
1401 |                 upperStr = unit.dateToString(upper);
1402 |             }
1403 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(tickLabelFont);
1404 |             double w1 = fm.stringWidth(lowerStr);
1405 |             double w2 = fm.stringWidth(upperStr);
1406 |             result += Math.max(w1, w2);
1407 |         }
1408 | 
1409 |         return result;
1410 | 
1411 |     }

/**
     * Tests two polygons for equality.  If both are <code>null</code> this 
     * method returns <code>true</code>.
     * 
     * @param p1  polygon 1 (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param p2  polygon 2 (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return A boolean.

Method code:
238 |     public static boolean equal(Polygon p1, Polygon p2) {
239 |         if (p1 == null) {
240 |             return (p2 == null);

242 |         if (p2 == null) {
243 |             return false;
244 |         }
245 |         if (p1.npoints != p2.npoints) {
246 |             return false;
247 |         }
248 |         if (!Arrays.equals(p1.xpoints, p2.xpoints)) {
249 |             return false;
250 |         }
251 |         if (!Arrays.equals(p1.ypoints, p2.ypoints)) {
252 |             return false;
253 |         }
254 |         return true;
255 |     }

     * Tests two maps containing (<code>Comparable</code>, <code>Paint</code>)
     * elements for equality.
     * 
     * @param map1  the first map (<code>null</code> not permitted).

1276 |     private boolean equalPaintMaps(Map map1, Map map2) {

Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextAnchor.java
Original range: 180-228

     * Ensures that serialization returns the unique instances.
     * 
     * @return The object.
     * 
     * @throws ObjectStreamException if there is a problem.
     */
Method code:
180 |     private Object readResolve() throws ObjectStreamException {
181 |         TextAnchor result = null;
182 |         if (this.equals(TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT)) {
183 |             result = TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT;

Signature: org.jfree.chart.JFreeChart.createAlignedRectangle2D(Size2D,Rectangle2D,HorizontalAlignment,VerticalAlignment)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/JFreeChart.java
Original range: 1231-1257
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a rectangle that is aligned to the frame.
     * 
     * @param dimensions
     * @param frame
     * @param hAlign
     * @param vAlign
     * 
     * @return A rectangle.
     */
Method code:
1231 |     private Rectangle2D createAlignedRectangle2D(Size2D dimensions, 
1232 |             Rectangle2D frame, HorizontalAlignment hAlign, 
1233 |             VerticalAlignment vAlign) {
1234 |         double x = Double.NaN;
1235 |         double y = Double.NaN;
1236 |         if (hAlign == HorizontalAlignment.LEFT) {
1237 |             x = frame.getX();   
1238 |         }
1239 |         else if (hAlign == HorizontalAlignment.CENTER) {
1240 |             x = frame.getCenterX() - (dimensions.width / 2.0);   
1241 |         }
1242 |         else if (hAlign == HorizontalAlignment.RIGHT) {
1243 |             x = frame.getMaxX() - dimensions.width;   
1244 |         }
1245 |         if (vAlign == VerticalAlignment.TOP) {
1246 |             y = frame.getY();   
1247 |         }
1248 |         else if (vAlign == VerticalAlignment.CENTER) {
1249 |             y = frame.getCenterY() - (dimensions.height / 2.0);   
1250 |         }
1251 |         else if (vAlign == VerticalAlignment.BOTTOM) {
1252 |             y = frame.getMaxY() - dimensions.height;   
1253 |         }
1254 |         
1255 |         return new Rectangle2D.Double(x, y, dimensions.width, 
1256 |                 dimensions.height);
1257 |     }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis3D.draw(Graphics2D,double,Rectangle2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge,PlotRenderingInfo)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis3D.java
Original range: 128-180
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the axis on a Java 2D graphics device (such as the screen or a 
     * printer).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param cursor  the cursor.
     * @param plotArea  the area for drawing the axes and data.
     * @param dataArea  the area for drawing the data (a subset of the 
     *                  plotArea).
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     * @param plotState  collects information about the plot (<code>null</code>
     *                   permitted).
     * 
     * @return The updated cursor value.
     */
Method code:
128 |     public AxisState draw(Graphics2D g2, double cursor, Rectangle2D plotArea, 
129 |             Rectangle2D dataArea, RectangleEdge edge, 
130 |             PlotRenderingInfo plotState) {
131 | 
132 |         // if the axis is not visible, don't draw it...
133 |         if (!isVisible()) {
134 |             AxisState state = new AxisState(cursor);
135 |             // even though the axis is not visible, we need ticks for the 
136 |             // gridlines...
137 |             List ticks = refreshTicks(g2, state, dataArea, edge); 
138 |             state.setTicks(ticks);
139 |             return state;
140 |         }
141 | 
142 |         // calculate the adjusted data area taking into account the 3D effect...
143 |         double xOffset = 0.0;
144 |         double yOffset = 0.0;
145 |         Plot plot = getPlot();
146 |         if (plot instanceof CategoryPlot) {
147 |             CategoryPlot cp = (CategoryPlot) plot;
148 |             CategoryItemRenderer r = cp.getRenderer();
149 |             if (r instanceof Effect3D) {
150 |                 Effect3D e3D = (Effect3D) r;
151 |                 xOffset = e3D.getXOffset();
152 |                 yOffset = e3D.getYOffset();
153 |             }
154 |         }
155 | 
156 |         double adjustedX = dataArea.getMinX();
157 |         double adjustedY = dataArea.getMinY();
158 |         double adjustedW = dataArea.getWidth() - xOffset;
159 |         double adjustedH = dataArea.getHeight() - yOffset;
160 | 
161 |         if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT || edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
162 |             adjustedY += yOffset;
163 |         }
164 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT || edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
165 |             adjustedX += xOffset;
166 |         }
167 |         Rectangle2D adjustedDataArea = new Rectangle2D.Double(adjustedX, 
168 |                 adjustedY, adjustedW, adjustedH);
169 | 
170 |         // draw the tick marks and labels...
171 |         AxisState info = drawTickMarksAndLabels(g2, cursor, plotArea, 
172 |                 adjustedDataArea, edge);
173 |        
174 |         // draw the axis label...
175 |         info = drawLabel(getLabel(), g2, plotArea, dataArea, edge, info, 
176 |                 plotState);
177 | 
178 |         return info;
179 |         
180 |     }

===== M0400 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.findStackedRangeBounds(CategoryDataset,KeyToGroupMap)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 1303-1355
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the minimum and maximum values for the dataset's range 
     * (y-values), assuming that the series in one category are stacked.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset.
     * @param map  a structure that maps series to groups.
     *
     * @return The value range (<code>null</code> if the dataset contains no 
     *         values).
     */
Method code:
1303 |     public static Range findStackedRangeBounds(CategoryDataset dataset,
1304 |                                                KeyToGroupMap map) {
1305 |     
1306 |         Range result = null;
1307 |         if (dataset != null) {
1308 |             
1309 |             // create an array holding the group indices...
1310 |             int[] groupIndex = new int[dataset.getRowCount()];
1311 |             for (int i = 0; i < dataset.getRowCount(); i++) {
1312 |                 groupIndex[i] = map.getGroupIndex(
1313 |                     map.getGroup(dataset.getRowKey(i))
1314 |                 );   
1315 |             }
1316 |             
1317 |             // minimum and maximum for each group...
1318 |             int groupCount = map.getGroupCount();
1319 |             double[] minimum = new double[groupCount];
1320 |             double[] maximum = new double[groupCount];
1321 |             
1322 |             int categoryCount = dataset.getColumnCount();
1323 |             for (int item = 0; item < categoryCount; item++) {
1324 |                 double[] positive = new double[groupCount];
1325 |                 double[] negative = new double[groupCount];
1326 |                 int seriesCount = dataset.getRowCount();
1327 |                 for (int series = 0; series < seriesCount; series++) {
1328 |                     Number number = dataset.getValue(series, item);
1329 |                     if (number != null) {
1330 |                         double value = number.doubleValue();
1331 |                         if (value > 0.0) {
1332 |                             positive[groupIndex[series]] 
1333 |                                  = positive[groupIndex[series]] + value;
1334 |                         }
1335 |                         if (value < 0.0) {
1336 |                             negative[groupIndex[series]] 
1337 |                                  = negative[groupIndex[series]] + value;
1338 |                                  // '+', remember value is negative
1339 |                         }
1340 |                     }
1341 |                 }
1342 |                 for (int g = 0; g < groupCount; g++) {
1343 |                     minimum[g] = Math.min(minimum[g], negative[g]);
1344 |                     maximum[g] = Math.max(maximum[g], positive[g]);
1345 |                 }
1346 |             }
1347 |             for (int j = 0; j < groupCount; j++) {
1348 |                 result = Range.combine(
1349 |                     result, new Range(minimum[j], maximum[j])
1350 |                 );
1351 |             }
1352 |         }
1353 |         return result;
1354 | 
1355 |     }

Method code:
438 |     private Range trimToContentWidth(Range r) {
439 |         if (r == null) {
440 |             return null;   
441 |         }
442 |         double lowerBound = 0.0;
443 |         double upperBound = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

449 |         }
450 |         return new Range(lowerBound, upperBound);

/**
     * Returns a range that includes all the values in the specified 
     * <code>range</code> AND the specified <code>value</code>.
     * 
     * @param range  the range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param value  the value that must be included.
     * 
     * @return A range.
     * 
     * @since 1.0.1
     */
Method code:
227 |     public static Range expandToInclude(Range range, double value) {
228 |         if (range == null) {
229 |             return new Range(value, value);
230 |         }
231 |         if (value < range.getLowerBound()) {
232 |             return new Range(value, range.getUpperBound());
233 |         }
234 |         else if (value > range.getUpperBound()) {
235 |             return new Range(range.getLowerBound(), value);
236 |         }
237 |         else {
238 |             return range;
239 |         }
240 |     }

/**
     * Returns the bounds for the specified text.
     * 
     * @param text  the text (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param g2  the graphics context (not <code>null</code>).
     * @param fm  the font metrics (not <code>null</code>).
     * 
     * @return The text bounds (<code>null</code> if the <code>text</code> 
     *         argument is <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
286 |     public static Rectangle2D getTextBounds(String text, Graphics2D g2, 
287 |             FontMetrics fm) {
288 |         
289 |         final Rectangle2D bounds;
290 |         if (TextUtilities.useFontMetricsGetStringBounds) {
291 |             bounds = fm.getStringBounds(text, g2);
292 |             // getStringBounds() can return incorrect height for some Unicode
293 |             // characters...see bug parade 6183356, let's replace it with 
294 |             // something correct

304 |                     height);

===== M0452 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.DefaultDrawingSupplier.equalShapes(Shape[],Shape[])
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/DefaultDrawingSupplier.java
Original range: 404-420
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method for testing the equality of two arrays of shapes.
     * 
     * @param s1  the first array (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param s2  the second array (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
404 |     private boolean equalShapes(Shape[] s1, Shape[] s2) {
405 |         if (s1 == null) {
406 |             return s2 == null;   
407 |         }
408 |         if (s2 == null) {
409 |             return false;   
410 |         }
411 |         if (s1.length != s2.length) {
412 |             return false;   
413 |         }
414 |         for (int i = 0; i < s1.length; i++) {
415 |             if (!ShapeUtilities.equal(s1[i], s2[i])) {
416 |                 return false;   
417 |             }
418 |         }
419 |         return true;
420 |     }

===== M0464 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.SerialUtilities.writeShape(Shape,ObjectOutputStream)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/SerialUtilities.java
Original range: 356-425
Documentation:
/**
     * Serialises a <code>Shape</code> object.
     *
     * @param shape  the shape object (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param stream  the output stream (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @throws IOException if there is an I/O error.
     */
Method code:
356 |     public static void writeShape(Shape shape, ObjectOutputStream stream) 
357 |             throws IOException {
358 | 
359 |         if (stream == null) {
360 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'stream' argument.");   
361 |         }
362 |         if (shape != null) {
363 |             stream.writeBoolean(false);
364 |             if (shape instanceof Line2D) {
365 |                 Line2D line = (Line2D) shape;
366 |                 stream.writeObject(Line2D.class);
367 |                 stream.writeDouble(line.getX1());
368 |                 stream.writeDouble(line.getY1());
369 |                 stream.writeDouble(line.getX2());
370 |                 stream.writeDouble(line.getY2());
371 |             }
372 |             else if (shape instanceof Rectangle2D) {
373 |                 Rectangle2D rectangle = (Rectangle2D) shape;
374 |                 stream.writeObject(Rectangle2D.class);
375 |                 stream.writeDouble(rectangle.getX());
376 |                 stream.writeDouble(rectangle.getY());
377 |                 stream.writeDouble(rectangle.getWidth());
378 |                 stream.writeDouble(rectangle.getHeight());
379 |             }
380 |             else if (shape instanceof Ellipse2D) {
381 |                 Ellipse2D ellipse = (Ellipse2D) shape;
382 |                 stream.writeObject(Ellipse2D.class);
383 |                 stream.writeDouble(ellipse.getX());
384 |                 stream.writeDouble(ellipse.getY());
385 |                 stream.writeDouble(ellipse.getWidth());
386 |                 stream.writeDouble(ellipse.getHeight());
387 |             }
388 |             else if (shape instanceof Arc2D) {
389 |                 Arc2D arc = (Arc2D) shape;
390 |                 stream.writeObject(Arc2D.class);
391 |                 stream.writeDouble(arc.getX());
392 |                 stream.writeDouble(arc.getY());
393 |                 stream.writeDouble(arc.getWidth());
394 |                 stream.writeDouble(arc.getHeight());
395 |                 stream.writeDouble(arc.getAngleStart());
396 |                 stream.writeDouble(arc.getAngleExtent());
397 |                 stream.writeInt(arc.getArcType());
398 |             }
399 |             else if (shape instanceof GeneralPath) {
400 |                 stream.writeObject(GeneralPath.class);
401 |                 PathIterator pi = shape.getPathIterator(null);
402 |                 float[] args = new float[6];
403 |                 stream.writeBoolean(pi.isDone());
404 |                 while (!pi.isDone()) {
405 |                     int type = pi.currentSegment(args);
406 |                     stream.writeInt(type);
407 |                     // TODO: could write this to only stream the values
408 |                     // required for the segment type
409 |                     for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
410 |                         stream.writeFloat(args[i]);
411 |                     }
412 |                     stream.writeInt(pi.getWindingRule());
413 |                     pi.next();
414 |                     stream.writeBoolean(pi.isDone());
415 |                 }
416 |             }
417 |             else {
418 |                 stream.writeObject(shape.getClass());
419 |                 stream.writeObject(shape);
420 |             }
421 |         }
422 |         else {
423 |             stream.writeBoolean(true);
424 |         }
425 |     }

/**
     * Draws an axis line at the current cursor position and edge.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param cursor  the cursor position.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param edge  the edge.
     */
Method code:
517 |     protected void drawAxisLine(Graphics2D g2, double cursor,
518 |                                 Rectangle2D dataArea, RectangleEdge edge) {
519 |         Line2D axisLine = null;
520 |         if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
521 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(dataArea.getX(), cursor, 
522 |                     dataArea.getMaxX(), cursor);  
523 |         }
524 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
525 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(dataArea.getX(), cursor, 
526 |                     dataArea.getMaxX(), cursor);  
527 |         }
528 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
529 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(cursor, dataArea.getY(), cursor, 
530 |                     dataArea.getMaxY());  
531 |         }
532 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
533 |             axisLine = new Line2D.Double(cursor, dataArea.getY(), cursor, 
534 |                     dataArea.getMaxY());  
535 |         }
536 |         g2.setPaint(getAxisLinePaint());
537 |         g2.setStroke(getAxisLineStroke());
538 |         g2.draw(axisLine);
539 |         
540 |         boolean drawUpOrRight = false;  
541 |         boolean drawDownOrLeft = false;
542 |         if (this.positiveArrowVisible) {
543 |             if (this.inverted) {
544 |                 drawDownOrLeft = true;   
545 |             }
546 |             else {
547 |                 drawUpOrRight = true;   
548 |             }
549 |         }
550 |         if (this.negativeArrowVisible) {
551 |             if (this.inverted) {
552 |                 drawUpOrRight = true;   
553 |             }
554 |             else {
555 |                 drawDownOrLeft = true;   
556 |             }
557 |         }

562 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP || edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
563 |                 x = dataArea.getMaxX();
564 |                 y = cursor;
565 |                 arrow = this.rightArrow; 
566 |             }
567 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT 
568 |                     || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
569 |                 x = cursor;
570 |                 y = dataArea.getMinY();
571 |                 arrow = this.upArrow; 
572 |             }
573 | 
574 |             // draw the arrow...
575 |             AffineTransform transformer = new AffineTransform();
576 |             transformer.setToTranslation(x, y);
577 |             Shape shape = transformer.createTransformedShape(arrow);
578 |             g2.fill(shape);
579 |             g2.draw(shape);
580 |         }
581 |         
582 |         if (drawDownOrLeft) {
583 |             double x = 0.0;
584 |             double y = 0.0;
585 |             Shape arrow = null;
586 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP || edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
587 |                 x = dataArea.getMinX();
588 |                 y = cursor;
589 |                 arrow = this.leftArrow; 
590 |             }
591 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT 
592 |                     || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
593 |                 x = cursor;
594 |                 y = dataArea.getMaxY();
595 |                 arrow = this.downArrow; 
596 |             }
597 | 
598 |             // draw the arrow...
599 |             AffineTransform transformer = new AffineTransform();
600 |             transformer.setToTranslation(x, y);
601 |             Shape shape = transformer.createTransformedShape(arrow);
602 |             g2.fill(shape);
603 |             g2.draw(shape);
604 |         }
605 |         
606 |     }

Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/SerialUtilities.java
Original range: 183-209
Documentation:
/**
     * Reads a <code>Stroke</code> object that has been serialised by the
     * {@link SerialUtilities#writeStroke(Stroke, ObjectOutputStream)} method.
     *
     * @param stream  the input stream (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The stroke object (possibly <code>null</code>).
     *
     * @throws IOException  if there is an I/O problem.
     * @throws ClassNotFoundException  if there is a problem loading a class.
     */
Method code:
183 |     public static Stroke readStroke(ObjectInputStream stream)
184 |             throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {
185 | 
186 |         if (stream == null) {
187 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'stream' argument.");   
188 |         }
189 |         Stroke result = null;
190 |         boolean isNull = stream.readBoolean();
191 |         if (!isNull) {
192 |             Class c = (Class) stream.readObject();
193 |             if (c.equals(BasicStroke.class)) {
194 |                 float width = stream.readFloat();
195 |                 int cap = stream.readInt();
196 |                 int join = stream.readInt();
197 |                 float miterLimit = stream.readFloat();
198 |                 float[] dash = (float[]) stream.readObject();
199 |                 float dashPhase = stream.readFloat();
200 |                 result = new BasicStroke(width, cap, join, miterLimit, dash, 
201 |                         dashPhase);
202 |             }
203 |             else {
204 |                 result = (Stroke) stream.readObject();
205 |             }
206 |         }
207 |         return result;
208 | 
209 |     }

===== M0495 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.createTextBlock(String,Font,Paint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 114-149
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a {@link TextBlock} from a <code>String</code>.  Line breaks 
     * are added where the <code>String</code> contains '\n' characters.
     * 
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param font  the font.
     * @param paint  the paint.
     * 
     * @return A text block.
     */
Method code:
114 |     public static TextBlock createTextBlock(String text, Font font,
115 |                                             Paint paint) {
116 |         if (text == null) {
117 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'text' argument.");
118 |         }
119 |         TextBlock result = new TextBlock();
120 |         String input = text;
121 |         boolean moreInputToProcess = (text.length() > 0);
122 |         int start = 0;
123 |         while (moreInputToProcess) {
124 |             int index = input.indexOf("\n");
125 |             if (index > start) {
126 |                 String line = input.substring(start, index);
127 |                 if (index < input.length() - 1) {
128 |                     result.addLine(line, font, paint);
129 |                     input = input.substring(index + 1);
130 |                 }
131 |                 else {
132 |                     moreInputToProcess = false;
133 |                 }
134 |             }
135 |             else if (index == start) {
136 |                 if (index < input.length() - 1) {
137 |                     input = input.substring(index + 1);
138 |                 }
139 |                 else {
140 |                     moreInputToProcess = false;
141 |                 }
142 |             }
143 |             else {
144 |                 result.addLine(input, font, paint);
145 |                 moreInputToProcess = false;
146 |             }
147 |         }
148 |         return result;
149 |     }

===== M0498 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.iterateCategoryRangeBounds(CategoryDataset,boolean)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 802-838
Documentation:
/**
     * Iterates over the data item of the category dataset to find
     * the range bounds.
     * 
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param includeInterval  a flag that determines whether or not the
     *                         y-interval is taken into account.
     * 
     * @return The range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
802 |     public static Range iterateCategoryRangeBounds(CategoryDataset dataset, 
803 |             boolean includeInterval) {
804 |         double minimum = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
805 |         double maximum = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
806 |         boolean interval = includeInterval 
807 |                            && dataset instanceof IntervalCategoryDataset;
808 |         int rowCount = dataset.getRowCount();
809 |         int columnCount = dataset.getColumnCount();
810 |         for (int row = 0; row < rowCount; row++) {
811 |             for (int column = 0; column < columnCount; column++) {
812 |                 Number lvalue;
813 |                 Number uvalue;
814 |                 if (interval) {
815 |                     IntervalCategoryDataset icd 
816 |                         = (IntervalCategoryDataset) dataset;
817 |                     lvalue = icd.getStartValue(row, column);
818 |                     uvalue = icd.getEndValue(row, column);
819 |                 }
820 |                 else {
821 |                     lvalue = dataset.getValue(row, column);
822 |                     uvalue = lvalue;
823 |                 }
824 |                 if (lvalue != null) {
825 |                     minimum = Math.min(minimum, lvalue.doubleValue());
826 |                 }
827 |                 if (uvalue != null) {
828 |                     maximum = Math.max(maximum, uvalue.doubleValue());
829 |                 }
830 |             }
831 |         }
832 |         if (minimum == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
833 |             return null;
834 |         }
835 |         else {
836 |             return new Range(minimum, maximum);
837 |         }
838 |     }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.CategoryAxis.refreshTicks(Graphics2D,AxisState,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/CategoryAxis.java
Original range: 968-1029
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a temporary list of ticks that can be used when drawing the axis.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (used to get font measurements).
     * @param state  the axis state.
     * @param dataArea  the area inside the axes.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     * 
     * @return A list of ticks.
     */
Method code:
968 |     public List refreshTicks(Graphics2D g2, 
969 |                              AxisState state,
970 |                              Rectangle2D dataArea,
971 |                              RectangleEdge edge) {
972 | 
973 |         List ticks = new java.util.ArrayList();
974 |         
975 |         // sanity check for data area...
976 |         if (dataArea.getHeight() <= 0.0 || dataArea.getWidth() < 0.0) {
977 |             return ticks;
978 |         }
979 | 
980 |         CategoryPlot plot = (CategoryPlot) getPlot();
981 |         List categories = plot.getCategoriesForAxis(this);
982 |         double max = 0.0;
983 |                 
984 |         if (categories != null) {
985 |             CategoryLabelPosition position 
986 |                     = this.categoryLabelPositions.getLabelPosition(edge);
987 |             float r = this.maximumCategoryLabelWidthRatio;
988 |             if (r <= 0.0) {
989 |                 r = position.getWidthRatio();   
990 |             }
991 |                   
992 |             float l = 0.0f;
993 |             if (position.getWidthType() == CategoryLabelWidthType.CATEGORY) {
994 |                 l = (float) calculateCategorySize(categories.size(), dataArea, 
995 |                         edge);  
996 |             }
997 |             else {
998 |                 if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
999 |                     l = (float) dataArea.getWidth();   
1000 |                 }
1001 |                 else {
1002 |                     l = (float) dataArea.getHeight();   
1003 |                 }
1004 |             }
1005 |             int categoryIndex = 0;
1006 |             Iterator iterator = categories.iterator();
1007 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
1008 |                 Comparable category = (Comparable) iterator.next();
1009 |                 TextBlock label = createLabel(category, l * r, edge, g2);
1010 |                 if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP || edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
1011 |                     max = Math.max(max, calculateTextBlockHeight(label, 
1012 |                             position, g2));
1013 |                 }
1014 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT 
1015 |                         || edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
1016 |                     max = Math.max(max, calculateTextBlockWidth(label, 
1017 |                             position, g2));
1018 |                 }
1019 |                 Tick tick = new CategoryTick(category, label, 
1020 |                         position.getLabelAnchor(), position.getRotationAnchor(), 
1021 |                         position.getAngle());
1022 |                 ticks.add(tick);
1023 |                 categoryIndex = categoryIndex + 1;
1024 |             }
1025 |         }
1026 |         state.setMax(max);
1027 |         return ticks;
1028 |         
1029 |     }

     * tick units) without the labels overlapping.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area defined by the axes.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     */
Method code:
1067 |    protected void selectHorizontalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D g2,
1068 |                                                Rectangle2D dataArea,
1069 |                                                RectangleEdge edge) {
1070 | 
1071 |         double tickLabelWidth = estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(
1072 |             g2, getTickUnit()
1073 |         );
1074 | 
1075 |         // start with the current tick unit...
1076 |         TickUnitSource tickUnits = getStandardTickUnits();
1077 |         TickUnit unit1 = tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(getTickUnit());
1078 |         double unit1Width = lengthToJava2D(unit1.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1079 | 
1080 |         // then extrapolate...
1081 |         double guess = (tickLabelWidth / unit1Width) * unit1.getSize();
1082 | 
1083 |         NumberTickUnit unit2 
1084 |             = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(guess);
1085 |         double unit2Width = lengthToJava2D(unit2.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1086 | 
1087 |         tickLabelWidth = estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(g2, unit2);
1088 |         if (tickLabelWidth > unit2Width) {
1089 |             unit2 = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getLargerTickUnit(unit2);
1090 |         }
1091 | 
1092 |         setTickUnit(unit2, false, false);
1093 | 
1094 |     }

/**
     * Validates the object. We use this opportunity to call listeners who have 
     * registered during the deserialization process, as listeners are not 
     * serialized. This method is called by the serialization system after the 
     * entire graph is read.
     *  
     * This object has registered itself to the system with a priority of 10. 
     * Other callbacks may register with a higher priority number to be called 
     * before this object, or with a lower priority number to be called after 
     * the listeners were notified.
     * 
     * All listeners are supposed to have register by now, either in their 
     * readObject or validateObject methods. Notify them that this dataset has 
     * changed.  
     *
     * @exception InvalidObjectException If the object cannot validate itself.

/**
     * Calculates the positions of the tick labels for the axis, storing the 
     * results in the tick label list (ready for drawing).
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the data should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the location of the axis.
     * 
     * @return A list of ticks.
     */
Method code:
1170 |     protected List refreshTicksHorizontal(Graphics2D g2,
1171 |                                           Rectangle2D dataArea,
1172 |                                           RectangleEdge edge) {
1173 | 
1174 |         List result = new java.util.ArrayList();
1175 | 
1176 |         Font tickLabelFont = getTickLabelFont();
1177 |         g2.setFont(tickLabelFont);
1178 |         
1179 |         if (isAutoTickUnitSelection()) {
1180 |             selectAutoTickUnit(g2, dataArea, edge);
1181 |         }
1182 | 
1183 |         double size = getTickUnit().getSize();
1184 |         int count = calculateVisibleTickCount();
1185 |         double lowestTickValue = calculateLowestVisibleTickValue();
1186 | 
1187 |         if (count <= ValueAxis.MAXIMUM_TICK_COUNT) {
1188 |             for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
1189 |                 double currentTickValue = lowestTickValue + (i * size);
1190 |                 String tickLabel;
1191 |                 NumberFormat formatter = getNumberFormatOverride();
1192 |                 if (formatter != null) {
1193 |                     tickLabel = formatter.format(currentTickValue);
1194 |                 }
1195 |                 else {
1196 |                     tickLabel = getTickUnit().valueToString(currentTickValue);
1197 |                 }
1198 |                 TextAnchor anchor = null;
1199 |                 TextAnchor rotationAnchor = null;
1200 |                 double angle = 0.0;
1201 |                 if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1202 |                     anchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1203 |                     rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT;
1204 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1205 |                         angle = Math.PI / 2.0;
1206 |                     }
1207 |                     else {
1208 |                         angle = -Math.PI / 2.0;
1209 |                     }
1210 |                 }
1211 |                 else {
1212 |                     if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
1213 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1214 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER;
1215 |                     }
1216 |                     else {
1217 |                         anchor = TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER;
1218 |                         rotationAnchor = TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER;
1219 |                     }
1220 |                 }
1221 | 
1222 |                 Tick tick = new NumberTick(new Double(currentTickValue), 
1223 |                         tickLabel, anchor, rotationAnchor, angle);
1224 |                 result.add(tick);
1225 |             }
1226 |         }
1227 |         return result;
1228 | 
1229 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.FlowArrangement.arrange(BlockContainer,Graphics2D,RectangleConstraint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/FlowArrangement.java
Original range: 126-166
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates and sets the bounds of all the items in the specified 
     * container, subject to the given constraint.  The <code>Graphics2D</code>
     * can be used by some items (particularly items containing text) to 
     * calculate sizing parameters.
     * 
     * @param container  the container whose items are being arranged.
     * @param constraint  the size constraint.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The size of the container after arrangement of the contents.
     */
Method code:
126 |     public Size2D arrange(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2,
127 |                           RectangleConstraint constraint) {
128 |         
129 |         LengthConstraintType w = constraint.getWidthConstraintType();
130 |         LengthConstraintType h = constraint.getHeightConstraintType();
131 |         if (w == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
132 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
133 |                 return arrangeNN(container, g2);  
134 |             }
135 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
136 |                 return arrangeNF(container, g2, constraint);  
137 |             }
138 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
139 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
140 |             }
141 |         }
142 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
143 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
144 |                 return arrangeFN(container, g2, constraint);  
145 |             }
146 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
147 |                 return arrangeFF(container, g2, constraint);  
148 |             }
149 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
150 |                 return arrangeFR(container, g2, constraint);  
151 |             }
152 |         }
153 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
154 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
155 |                 return arrangeRN(container, g2, constraint);  
156 |             }
157 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
158 |                 return arrangeRF(container, g2, constraint);  
159 |             }
160 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
161 |                 return arrangeRR(container, g2, constraint);   
162 |             }
163 |         }
164 |         throw new RuntimeException("Unrecognised constraint type.");
165 |         
166 |     }

/**
     * Draws a the title horizontally within the specified area.  This method 
     * will be called from the {@link #draw(Graphics2D, Rectangle2D) draw} 
     * method.

     * @param g2  the graphics device.

605 |         g2.setFont(this.font);

634 |             }
635 |             else if (horizontalAlignment == HorizontalAlignment.RIGHT) {

/**
     * Generates an array of keys, by appending a space plus an integer
     * (starting with 1) to the supplied prefix string.
     *
     * @param count  the number of keys required.
     * @param prefix  the name prefix.
     *
     * @return An array of <i>prefixN</i> with N = { 1 .. count}.
     */
Method code:
586 |     private Comparable[] generateKeys(int count, String prefix) {
587 |         Comparable[] result = new Comparable[count];

590 |             name = prefix + (i + 1);

593 |         return result;

Original range: 851-956

/**
     * Draws the category labels and returns the updated axis state.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param plotArea  the plot area (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param dataArea  the area inside the axes (<code>null</code> not

     * @param edge  the axis location (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param state  the axis state (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param plotState  collects information about the plot (<code>null</code>
     *                   permitted).
     * 
     * @return The updated axis state (never <code>null</code>).
     * 
     * @since 1.0.2
     */
Method code:
851 |     protected AxisState drawCategoryLabels(Graphics2D g2,
852 |                                            Rectangle2D plotArea,
853 |                                            Rectangle2D dataArea,
854 |                                            RectangleEdge edge,
855 |                                            AxisState state,
856 |                                            PlotRenderingInfo plotState) {
857 | 
858 |         if (state == null) {
859 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'state' argument.");
860 |         }

864 |             state.setTicks(ticks);

867 |             Iterator iterator = ticks.iterator();
868 |             while (iterator.hasNext()) {
869 |

872 |                 g2.setPaint(getTickLabelPaint(tick.getCategory()));

883 |                     x1 = getCategoryEnd(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), dataArea, 
884 |                             edge);

886 |                     y0 = y1 - state.getMax();
887 |                 }
888 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
889 |                     x0 = getCategoryStart(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), 
890 |                             dataArea, edge);
891 |                     x1 = getCategoryEnd(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), dataArea, 
892 |                             edge); 
893 |                     y0 = state.getCursor() + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
894 |                     y1 = y0 + state.getMax();
895 |                 }
896 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
897 |                     y0 = getCategoryStart(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), 
898 |                             dataArea, edge);
899 |                     y1 = getCategoryEnd(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), dataArea, 
900 |                             edge);
901 |                     x1 = state.getCursor() - this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
902 |                     x0 = x1 - state.getMax();
903 |                 }
904 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
905 |                     y0 = getCategoryStart(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), 
906 |                             dataArea, edge);
907 |                     y1 = getCategoryEnd(categoryIndex, ticks.size(), dataArea, 
908 |                             edge);
909 |                     x0 = state.getCursor() + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
910 |                     x1 = x0 - state.getMax();
911 |                 }
912 |                 Rectangle2D area = new Rectangle2D.Double(x0, y0, (x1 - x0), 
913 |                         (y1 - y0));
914 |                 Point2D anchorPoint = RectangleAnchor.coordinates(area, 
915 |                         position.getCategoryAnchor());
916 |                 TextBlock block = tick.getLabel();
917 |                 block.draw(g2, (float) anchorPoint.getX(), 
918 |                         (float) anchorPoint.getY(), position.getLabelAnchor(), 
919 |                         (float) anchorPoint.getX(), (float) anchorPoint.getY(), 
920 |                         position.getAngle());
921 |                 Shape bounds = block.calculateBounds(g2, 
922 |                         (float) anchorPoint.getX(), (float) anchorPoint.getY(), 
923 |                         position.getLabelAnchor(), (float) anchorPoint.getX(), 
924 |                         (float) anchorPoint.getY(), position.getAngle());
925 |                 if (plotState != null && plotState.getOwner() != null) {
926 |                     EntityCollection entities 
927 |                             = plotState.getOwner().getEntityCollection();
928 |                     if (entities != null) {
929 |                         String tooltip = getCategoryLabelToolTip(
930 |                                 tick.getCategory());
931 |                         entities.add(new CategoryLabelEntity(tick.getCategory(),
932 |                                 bounds, tooltip, null));
933 |                     }
934 |                 }
935 |                 categoryIndex++;
936 |             }
937 | 
938 |             if (edge.equals(RectangleEdge.TOP)) {
939 |                 double h = state.getMax() + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
940 |                 state.cursorUp(h);
941 |             }
942 |             else if (edge.equals(RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
943 |                 double h = state.getMax() + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
944 |                 state.cursorDown(h);
945 |             }
946 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
947 |                 double w = state.getMax() + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
948 |                 state.cursorLeft(w);
949 |             }
950 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
951 |                 double w = state.getMax() + this.categoryLabelPositionOffset;
952 |                 state.cursorRight(w);
953 |             }
954 |         }
955 |         return state;
956 |     }

===== M0575 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.Range.combine(Range,Range)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/Range.java
Original range: 198-214
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new range by combining two existing ranges.
     * <P>
     * Note that:
     * <ul>
     *   <li>either range can be <code>null</code>, in which case the other 
     *       range is returned;</li>
     *   <li>if both ranges are <code>null</code> the return value is 
     *       <code>null</code>.</li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * @param range1  the first range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param range2  the second range (<code>null</code> permitted).
     *
     * @return A new range (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
198 |     public static Range combine(Range range1, Range range2) {
199 |         if (range1 == null) {
200 |             return range2;
201 |         }
202 |         else {
203 |             if (range2 == null) {
204 |                 return range1;
205 |             }
206 |             else {
207 |                 double l = Math.min(range1.getLowerBound(), 
208 |                         range2.getLowerBound());
209 |                 double u = Math.max(range1.getUpperBound(), 
210 |                         range2.getUpperBound());
211 |                 return new Range(l, u);
212 |             }
213 |         }
214 |     }

/**
     * Creates a {@link CategoryDataset} that contains a copy of the data in 
     * an array.
     * <p>
     * Row and column keys are created by appending 0, 1, 2, ... to the 
     * supplied prefixes.
     *
     * @param rowKeyPrefix  the row key prefix.
     * @param columnKeyPrefix  the column key prefix.
     * @param data  the data.
     *
     * @return The dataset.
     */
Method code:
374 |     public static CategoryDataset createCategoryDataset(String rowKeyPrefix,
375 |                                                         String columnKeyPrefix,
376 |                                                         Number[][] data) {
377 | 
378 |         DefaultCategoryDataset result = new DefaultCategoryDataset();
379 |         for (int r = 0; r < data.length; r++) {
380 |             String rowKey = rowKeyPrefix + (r + 1);
381 |             for (int c = 0; c < data[r].length; c++) {
382 |                 String columnKey = columnKeyPrefix + (c + 1);
383 |                 result.addValue(data[r][c], rowKey, columnKey);
384 |             }
385 |         }
386 |         return result;
387 | 
388 |     }

/**
     * Sets a renderer.  A {@link PlotChangeEvent} is sent to all registered 
     * listeners.
     *
     * @param index  the index.
     * @param renderer  the renderer (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param notify  notify listeners?
     * 
     * @see #getRenderer(int)
     */
Method code:
1343 |     public void setRenderer(int index, CategoryItemRenderer renderer, 
1344 |                             boolean notify) {

1347 |         CategoryItemRenderer existing

===== M0606 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.autoAdjustRange()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 420-506
Documentation:
/**
     * Rescales the axis to ensure that all data is visible.
     */
Method code:
420 |     protected void autoAdjustRange() {
421 | 
422 |         Plot plot = getPlot();
423 |         if (plot == null) {
424 |             return;  // no plot, no data
425 |         }
426 | 
427 |         if (plot instanceof ValueAxisPlot) {
428 |             ValueAxisPlot vap = (ValueAxisPlot) plot;
429 | 
430 |             Range r = vap.getDataRange(this);
431 |             if (r == null) {
432 |                 r = getDefaultAutoRange();
433 |             }
434 |             
435 |             double upper = r.getUpperBound();
436 |             double lower = r.getLowerBound();
437 |             if (this.rangeType == RangeType.POSITIVE) {
438 |                 lower = Math.max(0.0, lower);
439 |                 upper = Math.max(0.0, upper);
440 |             }
441 |             else if (this.rangeType == RangeType.NEGATIVE) {
442 |                 lower = Math.min(0.0, lower);
443 |                 upper = Math.min(0.0, upper);                   
444 |             }
445 |             
446 |             if (getAutoRangeIncludesZero()) {
447 |                 lower = Math.min(lower, 0.0);
448 |                 upper = Math.max(upper, 0.0);
449 |             }
450 |             double range = upper - lower;
451 | 
452 |             // if fixed auto range, then derive lower bound...
453 |             double fixedAutoRange = getFixedAutoRange();
454 |             if (fixedAutoRange > 0.0) {
455 |                 lower = upper - fixedAutoRange;
456 |             }
457 |             else {
458 |                 // ensure the autorange is at least <minRange> in size...
459 |                 double minRange = getAutoRangeMinimumSize();
460 |                 if (range < minRange) {
461 |                     double expand = (minRange - range) / 2;
462 |                     upper = upper + expand;
463 |                     lower = lower - expand;
464 |                     if (lower == upper) { // see bug report 1549218
465 |                         double adjust = Math.abs(lower) / 10.0;
466 |                         lower = lower - adjust;
467 |                         upper = upper + adjust;
468 |                     }
469 |                     if (this.rangeType == RangeType.POSITIVE) {
470 |                         if (lower < 0.0) {
471 |                             upper = upper - lower;
472 |                             lower = 0.0;
473 |                         }
474 |                     }
475 |                     else if (this.rangeType == RangeType.NEGATIVE) {
476 |                         if (upper > 0.0) {
477 |                             lower = lower - upper;
478 |                             upper = 0.0;
479 |                         }
480 |                     }
481 |                 }
482 | 
483 |                 if (getAutoRangeStickyZero()) {
484 |                     if (upper <= 0.0) {
485 |                         upper = Math.min(0.0, upper + getUpperMargin() * range);
486 |                     }
487 |                     else {
488 |                         upper = upper + getUpperMargin() * range;
489 |                     }
490 |                     if (lower >= 0.0) {
491 |                         lower = Math.max(0.0, lower - getLowerMargin() * range);
492 |                     }
493 |                     else {
494 |                         lower = lower - getLowerMargin() * range;
495 |                     }
496 |                 }
497 |                 else {
498 |                     upper = upper + getUpperMargin() * range;
499 |                     lower = lower - getLowerMargin() * range;
500 |                 }
501 |             }
502 | 
503 |             setRange(new Range(lower, upper), false, false);
504 |         }
505 | 
506 |     }

/**
     * Returns the legend items for the plot.  By default, this method creates 
     * a legend item for each series in each of the datasets.  You can change 
     * this behaviour by overriding this method.
     *
     * @return The legend items.
     */

1729 |             result = new LegendItemCollection();

1735 |                     CategoryItemRenderer renderer = getRenderer(datasetIndex);
1736 |                     if (renderer != null) {

1739 |                             LegendItem item = renderer.getLegendItem(
1740 |                                     datasetIndex, i);

1742 |                                 result.add(item);
1743 |                             }
1744 |                         }
1745 |                     }
1746 |                 }
1747 |             }
1748 |         }
1749 |         return result;
1750 |     }

===== M0612 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.selectVerticalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 1105-1130
Documentation:
/**
     * Selects an appropriate tick value for the axis.  The strategy is to
     * display as many ticks as possible (selected from an array of 'standard'
     * tick units) without the labels overlapping.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param dataArea  the area in which the plot should be drawn.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     */
Method code:
1105 |     protected void selectVerticalAutoTickUnit(Graphics2D g2, 
1106 |                                               Rectangle2D dataArea, 
1107 |                                               RectangleEdge edge) {
1108 | 
1109 |         double tickLabelHeight = estimateMaximumTickLabelHeight(g2);
1110 | 
1111 |         // start with the current tick unit...
1112 |         TickUnitSource tickUnits = getStandardTickUnits();
1113 |         TickUnit unit1 = tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(getTickUnit());
1114 |         double unitHeight = lengthToJava2D(unit1.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1115 | 
1116 |         // then extrapolate...
1117 |         double guess = (tickLabelHeight / unitHeight) * unit1.getSize();
1118 |         
1119 |         NumberTickUnit unit2 
1120 |             = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getCeilingTickUnit(guess);
1121 |         double unit2Height = lengthToJava2D(unit2.getSize(), dataArea, edge);
1122 | 
1123 |         tickLabelHeight = estimateMaximumTickLabelHeight(g2);
1124 |         if (tickLabelHeight > unit2Height) {
1125 |             unit2 = (NumberTickUnit) tickUnits.getLargerTickUnit(unit2);
1126 |         }
1127 | 
1128 |         setTickUnit(unit2, false, false);
1129 | 
1130 |     }

Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the starting coordinate for the specified category.
     *
     * @param category  the category.
     * @param categoryCount  the number of categories.
     * @param area  the data area.
     * @param edge  the axis location.
     *
     * @return The coordinate.
     * 
     * @see #getCategoryMiddle(int, int, Rectangle2D, RectangleEdge)
     * @see #getCategoryEnd(int, int, Rectangle2D, RectangleEdge)
     */
Method code:
588 |     public double getCategoryStart(int category, int categoryCount, 
589 |                                    Rectangle2D area,
590 |                                    RectangleEdge edge) {
591 | 
592 |         double result = 0.0;
593 |         if ((edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) || (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM)) {
594 |             result = area.getX() + area.getWidth() * getLowerMargin();
595 |         }
596 |         else if ((edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) 
597 |                 || (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT)) {

602 |         double categoryGapWidth = calculateCategoryGapSize(categoryCount, area,
603 |                 edge);
604 | 
605 |         result = result + category * (categorySize + categoryGapWidth);
606 |         return result;
607 |         
608 |     }

===== M0632 =====
Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.findCumulativeRangeBounds(CategoryDataset)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 1521-1552
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the range of values for a dataset where each item is the 
     * running total of the items for the current series.
     * 
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * 
     * @return The range.
     * 
     * @see #findRangeBounds(CategoryDataset)
     */
Method code:
1521 |     public static Range findCumulativeRangeBounds(CategoryDataset dataset) {
1522 |         
1523 |         if (dataset == null) {
1524 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'dataset' argument.");
1525 |         }
1526 |         
1527 |         boolean allItemsNull = true; // we'll set this to false if there is at 
1528 |                                      // least one non-null data item... 
1529 |         double minimum = 0.0;
1530 |         double maximum = 0.0;
1531 |         for (int row = 0; row < dataset.getRowCount(); row++) {
1532 |             double runningTotal = 0.0;
1533 |             for (int column = 0; column < dataset.getColumnCount() - 1; 
1534 |                  column++) {
1535 |                 Number n = dataset.getValue(row, column);
1536 |                 if (n != null) {
1537 |                     allItemsNull = false;
1538 |                     double value = n.doubleValue();
1539 |                     runningTotal = runningTotal + value;
1540 |                     minimum = Math.min(minimum, runningTotal);
1541 |                     maximum = Math.max(maximum, runningTotal);
1542 |                 }
1543 |             }    
1544 |         }
1545 |         if (!allItemsNull) {
1546 |             return new Range(minimum, maximum);
1547 |         }
1548 |         else {
1549 |             return null;
1550 |         }
1551 |         
1552 |     }

710 |         if (RectangleEdge.isTopOrBottom(edge)) {
711 |             double minX = area.getX();
712 |             double maxX = area.getMaxX();
713 |             if (isInverted()) {
714 |                 result = maxX + ((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) 
715 |                          * (minX - maxX);
716 |             }
717 |             else {
718 |                 result = minX + ((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) 
719 |                          * (maxX - minX);
720 |             }
721 |         }
722 |         else if (RectangleEdge.isLeftOrRight(edge)) {
723 |             double minY = area.getMinY();
724 |             double maxY = area.getMaxY();
725 |             if (isInverted()) {
726 |                 result = minY + (((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) 
727 |                          * (maxY - minY));
728 |             }
729 |             else {
730 |                 result = maxY - (((value - axisMin) / (axisMax - axisMin)) 
731 |                          * (maxY - minY));
732 |             }
733 |         }
734 |         return result;
735 | 
736 |     }

453 |     private Range trimToContentHeight(Range r) {
454 |         if (r == null) {
455 |             return null;   
456 |         }
457 |         double lowerBound = 0.0;
458 |         double upperBound = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

464 |         }

Original range: 237-254

/**
     * Provides serialization support.
     *
     * @param stream  the output stream.
     *
     * @throws IOException  if there is an I/O error.
     */
Method code:
237 |     private void writeObject(ObjectOutputStream stream) 
238 |             throws IOException {
239 | 
240 |         stream.defaultWriteObject();
241 |         int count = size();
242 |         stream.writeInt(count);
243 |         for (int i = 0; i < count; i++) {
244 |             final Object object = get(i);
245 |             if (object != null && object instanceof Serializable) {
246 |                 stream.writeInt(i);
247 |                 stream.writeObject(object);
248 |             }
249 |             else {
250 |                 stream.writeInt(-1);
251 |             }
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.CategoryPlot.draw(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D,Point2D,PlotState,PlotRenderingInfo)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/CategoryPlot.java
Original range: 2530-2651
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the plot on a Java 2D graphics device (such as the screen or a 
     * printer).
     * <P>
     * At your option, you may supply an instance of {@link PlotRenderingInfo}.
     * If you do, it will be populated with information about the drawing,
     * including various plot dimensions and tooltip info.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param area  the area within which the plot (including axes) should 
     *              be drawn.
     * @param anchor  the anchor point (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param parentState  the state from the parent plot, if there is one.
     * @param state  collects info as the chart is drawn (possibly 
     *               <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
2530 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area, 
2531 |                      Point2D anchor,
2532 |                      PlotState parentState,
2533 |                      PlotRenderingInfo state) {
2534 | 
2535 |         // if the plot area is too small, just return...
2536 |         boolean b1 = (area.getWidth() <= MINIMUM_WIDTH_TO_DRAW);
2537 |         boolean b2 = (area.getHeight() <= MINIMUM_HEIGHT_TO_DRAW);
2538 |         if (b1 || b2) {
2539 |             return;
2540 |         }
2541 | 
2542 |         // record the plot area...
2543 |         if (state == null) {
2544 |             // if the incoming state is null, no information will be passed
2545 |             // back to the caller - but we create a temporary state to record
2546 |             // the plot area, since that is used later by the axes
2547 |             state = new PlotRenderingInfo(null);
2548 |         }
2549 |         state.setPlotArea(area);
2550 | 
2551 |         // adjust the drawing area for the plot insets (if any)...
2552 |         RectangleInsets insets = getInsets();
2553 |         insets.trim(area);
2554 | 
2555 |         // calculate the data area...
2556 |         AxisSpace space = calculateAxisSpace(g2, area);
2557 |         Rectangle2D dataArea = space.shrink(area, null);
2558 |         this.axisOffset.trim(dataArea);
2559 | 
2560 |         state.setDataArea(dataArea);
2561 | 
2562 |         // if there is a renderer, it draws the background, otherwise use the 
2563 |         // default background...
2564 |         if (getRenderer() != null) {
2565 |             getRenderer().drawBackground(g2, this, dataArea);
2566 |         }
2567 |         else {
2568 |             drawBackground(g2, dataArea);
2569 |         }
2570 |        
2571 |         Map axisStateMap = drawAxes(g2, area, dataArea, state);
2572 | 
2573 |         // don't let anyone draw outside the data area
2574 |         Shape savedClip = g2.getClip();
2575 |         g2.clip(dataArea);
2576 | 
2577 |         drawDomainGridlines(g2, dataArea);
2578 | 
2579 |         AxisState rangeAxisState = (AxisState) axisStateMap.get(getRangeAxis());
2580 |         if (rangeAxisState == null) {
2581 |             if (parentState != null) {
2582 |                 rangeAxisState = (AxisState) parentState.getSharedAxisStates()
2583 |                         .get(getRangeAxis());
2584 |             }
2585 |         }
2586 |         if (rangeAxisState != null) {
2587 |             drawRangeGridlines(g2, dataArea, rangeAxisState.getTicks());
2588 |         }
2589 |         
2590 |         // draw the markers...
2591 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2592 |             drawDomainMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.BACKGROUND);
2593 |         }        
2594 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2595 |             drawRangeMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.BACKGROUND);
2596 |         }
2597 | 
2598 |         // now render data items...
2599 |         boolean foundData = false;
2600 | 
2601 |         // set up the alpha-transparency...
2602 |         Composite originalComposite = g2.getComposite();
2603 |         g2.setComposite(AlphaComposite.getInstance(
2604 |                 AlphaComposite.SRC_OVER, getForegroundAlpha()));
2605 | 
2606 |         DatasetRenderingOrder order = getDatasetRenderingOrder();
2607 |         if (order == DatasetRenderingOrder.FORWARD) {
2608 |             for (int i = 0; i < this.datasets.size(); i++) {
2609 |                 foundData = render(g2, dataArea, i, state) || foundData;
2610 |             }
2611 |         }
2612 |         else {  // DatasetRenderingOrder.REVERSE
2613 |             for (int i = this.datasets.size() - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
2614 |                 foundData = render(g2, dataArea, i, state) || foundData;   
2615 |             }
2616 |         }
2617 |         // draw the foreground markers...
2618 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2619 |             drawDomainMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.FOREGROUND);
2620 |         }
2621 |         for (int i = 0; i < this.renderers.size(); i++) {
2622 |             drawRangeMarkers(g2, dataArea, i, Layer.FOREGROUND);
2623 |         }
2624 | 
2625 |         // draw the annotations (if any)...
2626 |         drawAnnotations(g2, dataArea);
2627 | 
2628 |         g2.setClip(savedClip);
2629 |         g2.setComposite(originalComposite);
2630 | 
2631 |         if (!foundData) {
2632 |             drawNoDataMessage(g2, dataArea);
2633 |         }
2634 | 
2635 |         // draw range crosshair if required...
2636 |         if (isRangeCrosshairVisible()) {
2637 |             // FIXME: this doesn't handle multiple range axes
2638 |             drawRangeCrosshair(g2, dataArea, getOrientation(), 
2639 |                     getRangeCrosshairValue(), getRangeAxis(),
2640 |                     getRangeCrosshairStroke(), getRangeCrosshairPaint());
2641 |         }
2642 | 
2643 |         // draw an outline around the plot area...
2644 |         if (getRenderer() != null) {
2645 |             getRenderer().drawOutline(g2, this, dataArea);
2646 |         }
2647 |         else {
2648 |             drawOutline(g2, dataArea);
2649 |         }
2650 |

     * @param cursor  the cursor location.
     * @param plotArea  the area within which the axis should be drawn 
     *                  (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param dataArea  the area within which the plot is being drawn 
     *                  (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param edge  the location of the axis (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param plotState  collects information about the plot 
     *                   (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return The axis state (never <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
808 |     public AxisState draw(Graphics2D g2, 
809 |                           double cursor, 
810 |                           Rectangle2D plotArea,

813 |                           PlotRenderingInfo plotState) {

829 |                 plotState);
830 |     
831 |         return state;
832 |
