/**
     * Constructs a DefaultIntervalCategoryDataset, populates it with data
     * from the arrays, and uses the supplied names for the series and the
     * supplied objects for the categories.
     *
     * @param seriesKeys the series keys.
     * @param categoryKeys  the categories.
     * @param starts  the start values data, indexed as data[series][category].
     * @param ends  the end values data, indexed as data[series][category].
     */
Method code:
136 |     public DefaultIntervalCategoryDataset(Comparable[] seriesKeys,
137 |                                           Comparable[] categoryKeys,
138 |                                           Number[][] starts,
139 |                                           Number[][] ends) {
140 |

142 |         this.endData = ends;

144 |         if (starts != null && ends != null) {

147 |             ResourceBundle resources = ResourceBundle.getBundle(baseName);

150 |             if (seriesCount != ends.length) {

152 |                     + "of series in the start value dataset does "

158 |

160 |                 if (seriesKeys != null) {

172 |                             "series.default-prefix") + " ";
173 |                     this.seriesKeys = generateKeys(seriesCount, prefix);
174 |                 }

177 |                 int categoryCount = starts[0].length;
178 |                 if (categoryCount != ends[0].length) {
179 |                     String errMsg = "DefaultIntervalCategoryDataset: the "
180 |                                 + "number of categories in the start value "
181 |                                 + "dataset does not match the number of "
182 |                                 + "categories in the end value dataset.";
183 |                     throw new IllegalArgumentException(errMsg);
184 |                 }

186 |                     if (categoryKeys.length != categoryCount) {
187 |                         throw new IllegalArgumentException(
188 |                                 "The number of category keys does not match "

190 |                     }

192 |                 }

197 |                 }

203 |             }

/**
     * Creates a stacked bar chart with a 3D effect and default settings. The 
     * chart object returned by this method uses a {@link CategoryPlot} 
     * instance as the plot, with a {@link CategoryAxis3D} for the domain axis, 
     * a {@link NumberAxis3D} as the range axis, and a 
     * {@link StackedBarRenderer3D} as the renderer.
     *
     * @param title  the chart title (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param categoryAxisLabel  the label for the category axis 
     *                           (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param valueAxisLabel  the label for the value axis (<code>null</code> 
     *                        permitted).
     * @param dataset  the dataset for the chart (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param orientation  the orientation (horizontal or vertical) 
     *                     (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param legend  a flag specifying whether or not a legend is required.
     * @param tooltips  configure chart to generate tool tips?
     * @param urls  configure chart to generate URLs?
     *
     * @return A stacked bar chart with a 3D effect.
     */
Method code:
771 |     public static JFreeChart createStackedBarChart3D(String title,
772 |                                                     String categoryAxisLabel,
773 |                                                     String valueAxisLabel,
774 |                                                     CategoryDataset dataset,
775 |                                                     PlotOrientation orientation,
776 |                                                     boolean legend,
777 |                                                     boolean tooltips,
778 |                                                     boolean urls) {
779 | 
780 |         if (orientation == null) {
781 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
782 |         }
783 |         CategoryAxis categoryAxis = new CategoryAxis3D(categoryAxisLabel);
784 |         ValueAxis valueAxis = new NumberAxis3D(valueAxisLabel);
785 | 
786 |         // create the renderer...
787 |         CategoryItemRenderer renderer = new StackedBarRenderer3D();
788 |         if (tooltips) {
789 |             renderer.setBaseToolTipGenerator(
790 |                     new StandardCategoryToolTipGenerator());
791 |         }
792 |         if (urls) {
793 |             renderer.setBaseURLGenerator(new StandardCategoryURLGenerator());
794 |         }
795 | 
796 |         // create the plot...
797 |         CategoryPlot plot = new CategoryPlot(dataset, categoryAxis, valueAxis, 
798 |                 renderer);
799 |         plot.setOrientation(orientation);
800 |         if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
801 |             // change rendering order to ensure that bar overlapping is the 
802 |             // right way around
803 |             plot.setColumnRenderingOrder(SortOrder.DESCENDING);
804 |         }
805 | 
806 |         // create the chart...
807 |         JFreeChart chart = new JFreeChart(title, JFreeChart.DEFAULT_TITLE_FONT, 
808 |                 plot, legend);
809 | 
810 |         return chart;
811 | 
812 |     }

===== M0709 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.NumberAxis.estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(Graphics2D,TickUnit)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/NumberAxis.java
Original range: 997-1034
Documentation:
/**
     * Estimates the maximum width of the tick labels, assuming the specified 
     * tick unit is used.
     * <P>
     * Rather than computing the string bounds of every tick on the axis, we 
     * just look at two values: the lower bound and the upper bound for the 
     * axis.  These two values will usually be representative.
     *
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param unit  the tick unit to use for calculation.
     *
     * @return The estimated maximum width of the tick labels.
     */
Method code:
997 |     protected double estimateMaximumTickLabelWidth(Graphics2D g2, 
998 |                                                    TickUnit unit) {
999 | 
1000 |         RectangleInsets tickLabelInsets = getTickLabelInsets();
1001 |         double result = tickLabelInsets.getLeft() + tickLabelInsets.getRight();
1002 | 
1003 |         if (isVerticalTickLabels()) {
1004 |             // all tick labels have the same width (equal to the height of the 
1005 |             // font)...
1006 |             FontRenderContext frc = g2.getFontRenderContext();
1007 |             LineMetrics lm = getTickLabelFont().getLineMetrics("0", frc);
1008 |             result += lm.getHeight();
1009 |         }
1010 |         else {
1011 |             // look at lower and upper bounds...
1012 |             FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(getTickLabelFont());
1013 |             Range range = getRange();
1014 |             double lower = range.getLowerBound();
1015 |             double upper = range.getUpperBound();
1016 |             String lowerStr = "";
1017 |             String upperStr = "";
1018 |             NumberFormat formatter = getNumberFormatOverride();
1019 |             if (formatter != null) {
1020 |                 lowerStr = formatter.format(lower);
1021 |                 upperStr = formatter.format(upper);
1022 |             }
1023 |             else {
1024 |                 lowerStr = unit.valueToString(lower);
1025 |                 upperStr = unit.valueToString(upper);                
1026 |             }
1027 |             double w1 = fm.stringWidth(lowerStr);
1028 |             double w2 = fm.stringWidth(upperStr);
1029 |             result += Math.max(w1, w2);
1030 |         }
1031 | 
1032 |         return result;
1033 | 
1034 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.BorderArrangement.arrangeNN(BlockContainer,Graphics2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/BorderArrangement.java
Original range: 186-260
Documentation:
/**
     * Performs an arrangement without constraints.
     * 
     * @param container  the container.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * 
     * @return The container size after the arrangement.
     */
Method code:
186 |     protected Size2D arrangeNN(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2) {
187 |         double[] w = new double[5];
188 |         double[] h = new double[5];
189 |         if (this.topBlock != null) {
190 |             Size2D size = this.topBlock.arrange(
191 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
192 |             );
193 |             w[0] = size.width;
194 |             h[0] = size.height;
195 |         }
196 |         if (this.bottomBlock != null) {
197 |             Size2D size = this.bottomBlock.arrange(
198 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
199 |             );
200 |             w[1] = size.width;
201 |             h[1] = size.height;
202 |         }
203 |         if (this.leftBlock != null) {
204 |             Size2D size = this.leftBlock.arrange(
205 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
206 |             );
207 |             w[2] = size.width;
208 |             h[2] = size.height;
209 |        }
210 |         if (this.rightBlock != null) {
211 |             Size2D size = this.rightBlock.arrange(
212 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
213 |             );
214 |             w[3] = size.width;
215 |             h[3] = size.height;
216 |         }
217 |         
218 |         h[2] = Math.max(h[2], h[3]);
219 |         h[3] = h[2];
220 |         
221 |         if (this.centerBlock != null) {
222 |             Size2D size = this.centerBlock.arrange(
223 |                 g2, RectangleConstraint.NONE
224 |             );
225 |             w[4] = size.width;
226 |             h[4] = size.height;
227 |         }
228 |         double width = Math.max(w[0], Math.max(w[1], w[2] + w[4] + w[3]));
229 |         double centerHeight = Math.max(h[2], Math.max(h[3], h[4]));
230 |         double height = h[0] + h[1] + centerHeight;
231 |         if (this.topBlock != null) {
232 |             this.topBlock.setBounds(
233 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(0.0, 0.0, width, h[0])
234 |             );
235 |         }
236 |         if (this.bottomBlock != null) {
237 |             this.bottomBlock.setBounds(
238 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(0.0, height - h[1], width, h[1])
239 |             );
240 |         }
241 |         if (this.leftBlock != null) {
242 |             this.leftBlock.setBounds(
243 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(0.0, h[0], w[2], centerHeight)
244 |             );
245 |         }
246 |         if (this.rightBlock != null) {
247 |             this.rightBlock.setBounds(
248 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(width - w[3], h[0], w[3], centerHeight)
249 |             );
250 |         }
251 |         
252 |         if (this.centerBlock != null) {
253 |             this.centerBlock.setBounds(
254 |                 new Rectangle2D.Double(
255 |                     w[2], h[0], width - w[2] - w[3], centerHeight
256 |                 )
257 |             );
258 |         }
259 |         return new Size2D(width, height);
260 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.plot.Plot.resolveRangeAxisLocation(AxisLocation,PlotOrientation)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/plot/Plot.java
Original range: 1364-1415
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolves a range axis location for a given plot orientation.
     *
     * @param location  the location (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param orientation  the orientation (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The edge (never <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
1364 |     public static RectangleEdge resolveRangeAxisLocation(
1365 |             AxisLocation location, PlotOrientation orientation) {
1366 | 
1367 |         if (location == null) {
1368 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'location' argument.");   
1369 |         }
1370 |         if (orientation == null) {
1371 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
1372 |         }
1373 | 
1374 |         RectangleEdge result = null;
1375 |         
1376 |         if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_RIGHT) {
1377 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1378 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1379 |             }
1380 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1381 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1382 |             }
1383 |         }
1384 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.TOP_OR_LEFT) {
1385 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1386 |                 result = RectangleEdge.TOP;
1387 |             }
1388 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1389 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1390 |             }
1391 |         }
1392 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_RIGHT) {
1393 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1394 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1395 |             }
1396 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1397 |                 result = RectangleEdge.RIGHT;
1398 |             }
1399 |         }
1400 |         else if (location == AxisLocation.BOTTOM_OR_LEFT) {
1401 |             if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
1402 |                 result = RectangleEdge.BOTTOM;
1403 |             }
1404 |             else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
1405 |                 result = RectangleEdge.LEFT;
1406 |             }
1407 |         }
1408 | 
1409 |         // the above should cover all the options...
1410 |         if (result == null) {
1411 |             throw new IllegalStateException("resolveRangeAxisLocation()");
1412 |         }
1413 |         return result;
1414 |         
1415 |     }

===== M0737 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.ShapeUtilities.equal(Shape,Shape)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/ShapeUtilities.java
Original range: 129-149
Documentation:
/**
     * Tests two shapes for equality.  If both shapes are <code>null</code>, 
     * this method will return <code>true</code>. 
     * <p>
     * In the current implementation, the following shapes are supported: 
     * <code>Ellipse2D</code>, <code>Line2D</code> and <code>Rectangle2D</code> 
     * (implicit).
     * 
     * @param s1  the first shape (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param s2  the second shape (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
129 |     public static boolean equal(Shape s1, Shape s2) {
130 |         if (s1 instanceof Line2D && s2 instanceof Line2D) {
131 |             return equal((Line2D) s1, (Line2D) s2);
132 |         }
133 |         else if (s1 instanceof Ellipse2D && s2 instanceof Ellipse2D) {
134 |             return equal((Ellipse2D) s1, (Ellipse2D) s2);
135 |         }
136 |         else if (s1 instanceof Arc2D && s2 instanceof Arc2D) {
137 |             return equal((Arc2D) s1, (Arc2D) s2);   
138 |         }
139 |         else if (s1 instanceof Polygon && s2 instanceof Polygon) {
140 |             return equal((Polygon) s1, (Polygon) s2);   
141 |         }
142 |         else if (s1 instanceof GeneralPath && s2 instanceof GeneralPath) {
143 |             return equal((GeneralPath) s1, (GeneralPath) s2);   
144 |         }
145 |         else {
146 |             // this will handle Rectangle2D...
147 |             return ObjectUtilities.equal(s1, s2);
148 |         }
149 |     }

===== M0755 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.ValueAxis.calculateAnchorPoint(ValueTick,double,Rectangle2D,RectangleEdge)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/ValueAxis.java
Original range: 618-642
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates the anchor point for a tick label.
     * 
     * @param tick  the tick.
     * @param cursor  the cursor.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.
     * @param edge  the edge on which the axis is drawn.
     * 
     * @return The x and y coordinates of the anchor point.
     */
Method code:
618 |     protected float[] calculateAnchorPoint(ValueTick tick, 
619 |                                            double cursor, 
620 |                                            Rectangle2D dataArea, 
621 |                                            RectangleEdge edge) {
622 |     
623 |         RectangleInsets insets = getTickLabelInsets();
624 |         float[] result = new float[2];
625 |         if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
626 |             result[0] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
627 |             result[1] = (float) (cursor - insets.getBottom() - 2.0);
628 |         }
629 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
630 |             result[0] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
631 |             result[1] = (float) (cursor + insets.getTop() + 2.0); 
632 |         }
633 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
634 |             result[0] = (float) (cursor - insets.getLeft() - 2.0);    
635 |             result[1] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
636 |         }
637 |         else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
638 |             result[0] = (float) (cursor + insets.getRight() + 2.0);    
639 |             result[1] = (float) valueToJava2D(tick.getValue(), dataArea, edge);
640 |         }
641 |         return result;
642 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.renderer.category.BoxAndWhiskerRenderer.getLegendItem(int,int)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/renderer/category/BoxAndWhiskerRenderer.java
Original range: 226-264
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns a legend item for a series.
     *
     * @param datasetIndex  the dataset index (zero-based).
     * @param series  the series index (zero-based).
     *
     * @return The legend item (possibly <code>null</code>).
     */
Method code:
226 |     public LegendItem getLegendItem(int datasetIndex, int series) {
227 | 
228 |         CategoryPlot cp = getPlot();
229 |         if (cp == null) {
230 |             return null;
231 |         }
232 | 
233 |         // check that a legend item needs to be displayed...
234 |         if (!isSeriesVisible(series) || !isSeriesVisibleInLegend(series)) {
235 |             return null;
236 |         }
237 | 
238 |         CategoryDataset dataset = cp.getDataset(datasetIndex);
239 |         String label = getLegendItemLabelGenerator().generateLabel(dataset, 
240 |                 series);
241 |         String description = label;
242 |         String toolTipText = null; 
243 |         if (getLegendItemToolTipGenerator() != null) {
244 |             toolTipText = getLegendItemToolTipGenerator().generateLabel(
245 |                     dataset, series);   
246 |         }
247 |         String urlText = null;
248 |         if (getLegendItemURLGenerator() != null) {
249 |             urlText = getLegendItemURLGenerator().generateLabel(dataset, 
250 |                     series);   
251 |         }
252 |         Shape shape = new Rectangle2D.Double(-4.0, -4.0, 8.0, 8.0);
253 |         Paint paint = lookupSeriesPaint(series);
254 |         Paint outlinePaint = lookupSeriesOutlinePaint(series);
255 |         Stroke outlineStroke = lookupSeriesOutlineStroke(series);
256 |         LegendItem result = new LegendItem(label, description, toolTipText, 
257 |                 urlText, shape, paint, outlineStroke, outlinePaint);
258 |         result.setDataset(dataset);
259 |         result.setDatasetIndex(datasetIndex);
260 |         result.setSeriesKey(dataset.getRowKey(series));
261 |         result.setSeriesIndex(series);
262 |         return result;
263 | 
264 |     }

     * the margin, border and padding space from the specified fixed 
     * height.

===== M0773 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.nextLineBreak(String,int,float,BreakIterator,TextMeasurer)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 236-274
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the character index of the next line break.
     * 
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param start  the start index.
     * @param width  the target display width.
     * @param iterator  the word break iterator.
     * @param measurer  the text measurer.
     * 
     * @return The index of the next line break.
     */
Method code:
236 |     private static int nextLineBreak(String text, int start,
237 |             float width, BreakIterator iterator, TextMeasurer measurer) {
238 |         
239 |         // this method is (loosely) based on code in JFreeReport's 
240 |         // TextParagraph class
241 |         int current = start;
242 |         int end;
243 |         float x = 0.0f;
244 |         boolean firstWord = true;
245 |         int newline = text.indexOf('\n', start);
246 |         if (newline < 0) {
247 |             newline = Integer.MAX_VALUE;
248 |         }
249 |         while (((end = iterator.next()) != BreakIterator.DONE)) {
250 |             if (end > newline) {
251 |                 return newline;
252 |             }
253 |             x += measurer.getStringWidth(text, current, end);
254 |             if (x > width) {
255 |                 if (firstWord) {
256 |                     while (measurer.getStringWidth(text, start, end) > width) {
257 |                         end--;
258 |                         if (end <= start) {
259 |                             return end;
260 |                         }
261 |                     }
262 |                     return end;
263 |                 }
264 |                 else {
265 |                     end = iterator.previous();
266 |                     return end;
267 |                 }
268 |             }
269 |             // we found at least one word that fits ...
270 |             firstWord = false;
271 |             current = end;
272 |         }
273 |         return BreakIterator.DONE;
274 |     }

/**
     * Adds a mean and standard deviation to the table.
     *
     * @param mean  the mean.
     * @param standardDeviation  the standard deviation.
     * @param rowKey  the row key.
     * @param columnKey  the column key.
     */
Method code:
297 |     public void add(Number mean, Number standardDeviation,
298 |                     Comparable rowKey, Comparable columnKey) {
299 |         MeanAndStandardDeviation item = new MeanAndStandardDeviation(
300 |                 mean, standardDeviation);
301 |         this.data.addObject(item, rowKey, columnKey);
302 |         double m = 0.0;
303 |         double sd = 0.0;
304 |         if (mean != null) {
305 |             m = mean.doubleValue();
306 |         }
307 |         if (standardDeviation != null) {
308 |             sd = standardDeviation.doubleValue();   
309 |         }
310 |         
311 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m)) {
312 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.maximumRangeValue) 
313 |                     || m > this.maximumRangeValue) {
314 |                 this.maximumRangeValue = m;
315 |             }
316 |         }
317 |         
318 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m + sd)) {
319 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.maximumRangeValueIncStdDev) 
320 |                     || (m + sd) > this.maximumRangeValueIncStdDev) {
321 |                 this.maximumRangeValueIncStdDev = m + sd;
322 |             }
323 |         }
324 | 
325 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m)) {
326 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.minimumRangeValue) 
327 |                     || m < this.minimumRangeValue) {
328 |                 this.minimumRangeValue = m;
329 |             }
330 |         }
331 | 
332 |         if (!Double.isNaN(m - sd)) {
333 |             if (Double.isNaN(this.minimumRangeValueIncStdDev) 
334 |                     || (m - sd) < this.minimumRangeValueIncStdDev) {
335 |                 this.minimumRangeValueIncStdDev = m - sd;
336 |             }
337 |         }
338 | 
339 |         fireDatasetChanged();
340 |     }

     * axis.  If the dataset is <code>null</code>, this method returns

     * @param axis  the axis.

3066 |     public Range getDataRange(ValueAxis axis) {
3067 | 
3068 |         Range result = null;
3069 |         List mappedDatasets = new ArrayList();
3070 |

3072 |         if (rangeIndex >= 0) {
3073 |             mappedDatasets.addAll(datasetsMappedToRangeAxis(rangeIndex));

3088 |         }

Signature: org.jfree.data.general.DatasetUtilities.findStackedRangeBounds(CategoryDataset,double)
Source: source/org/jfree/data/general/DatasetUtilities.java
Original range: 1257-1291
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns the minimum and maximum values for the dataset's range 
     * (y-values), assuming that the series in one category are stacked.
     *
     * @param dataset  the dataset (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param base  the base value for the bars.
     *
     * @return The range (<code>null</code> if the dataset contains no values).
     */
Method code:
1257 |     public static Range findStackedRangeBounds(CategoryDataset dataset, 
1258 |             double base) {
1259 |         if (dataset == null) {
1260 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'dataset' argument.");
1261 |         }
1262 |         Range result = null;
1263 |         double minimum = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
1264 |         double maximum = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
1265 |         int categoryCount = dataset.getColumnCount();
1266 |         for (int item = 0; item < categoryCount; item++) {
1267 |             double positive = base;
1268 |             double negative = base;
1269 |             int seriesCount = dataset.getRowCount();
1270 |             for (int series = 0; series < seriesCount; series++) {
1271 |                 Number number = dataset.getValue(series, item);
1272 |                 if (number != null) {
1273 |                     double value = number.doubleValue();
1274 |                     if (value > 0.0) {
1275 |                         positive = positive + value;
1276 |                     }
1277 |                     if (value < 0.0) {
1278 |                         negative = negative + value;  
1279 |                         // '+', remember value is negative
1280 |                     }
1281 |                 }
1282 |             }
1283 |             minimum = Math.min(minimum, negative);
1284 |             maximum = Math.max(maximum, positive);
1285 |         }
1286 |         if (minimum <= maximum) {
1287 |             result = new Range(minimum, maximum);
1288 |         }
1289 |         return result;
1290 | 
1291 |     }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.PaintUtilities.equal(Paint,Paint)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/util/PaintUtilities.java
Original range: 75-102
Documentation:
/**
     * Returns <code>true</code> if the two <code>Paint</code> objects are equal 
     * OR both <code>null</code>.  This method handles
     * <code>GradientPaint</code> as a special case.
     *
     * @param p1  paint 1 (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param p2  paint 2 (<code>null</code> permitted).
     *
     * @return A boolean.
     */
Method code:
75 |     public static boolean equal(Paint p1, Paint p2) {
76 | 
77 |         // handle cases where either or both arguments are null
78 |         if (p1 == null) {
79 |             return (p2 == null);   
80 |         }
81 |         if (p2 == null) {
82 |             return false;   
83 |         }
84 |         
85 |         boolean result = false;
86 |         // handle GradientPaint as a special case...
87 |         if (p1 instanceof GradientPaint && p2 instanceof GradientPaint) {
88 |             GradientPaint gp1 = (GradientPaint) p1;
89 |             GradientPaint gp2 = (GradientPaint) p2;
90 |             result = gp1.getColor1().equals(gp2.getColor1()) 
91 |                 && gp1.getColor2().equals(gp2.getColor2())
92 |                 && gp1.getPoint1().equals(gp2.getPoint1())    
93 |                 && gp1.getPoint2().equals(gp2.getPoint2())
94 |                 && gp1.isCyclic() == gp2.isCyclic()
95 |                 && gp1.getTransparency() == gp1.getTransparency(); 
96 |         }
97 |         else {
98 |             result = p1.equals(p2);
99 |         }
100 |         return result;
101 | 
102 |     }

     * Tests the list for equality with another object (typically also a list).

Method code:
93 |     public boolean equals(Object obj) {
94 |         if (obj == null) {
95 |             return false;
96 |         }
97 |         if (obj == this) {
98 |             return true;
99 |         }

Signature: org.jfree.chart.util.RectangleInsets.createOutsetRectangle(Rectangle2D,boolean,boolean)

Original range: 324-348
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates an outset rectangle.
     * 
     * @param base  the base rectangle (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param horizontal  apply horizontal insets?
     * @param vertical  apply vertical insets? 
     * 
     * @return An outset rectangle.
     */
Method code:
324 |     public Rectangle2D createOutsetRectangle(Rectangle2D base,
325 |                                              boolean horizontal, 
326 |                                              boolean vertical) {
327 |         if (base == null) {
328 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'base' argument.");
329 |         }
330 |         double topMargin = 0.0;
331 |         double bottomMargin = 0.0;
332 |         if (vertical) {
333 |             topMargin = calculateTopOutset(base.getHeight());
334 |             bottomMargin = calculateBottomOutset(base.getHeight());
335 |         }
336 |         double leftMargin = 0.0;
337 |         double rightMargin = 0.0;
338 |         if (horizontal) {
339 |             leftMargin = calculateLeftOutset(base.getWidth());
340 |             rightMargin = calculateRightOutset(base.getWidth());
341 |         }
342 |         return new Rectangle2D.Double(
343 |             base.getX() - leftMargin, 
344 |             base.getY() - topMargin,
345 |             base.getWidth() + leftMargin + rightMargin,
346 |             base.getHeight() + topMargin + bottomMargin
347 |         );
348 |     }

     * Draws a string that is aligned by one anchor point and rotated about

     *

===== M0813 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.text.TextUtilities.drawRotatedString(String,Graphics2D,float,float,double,float,float)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/text/TextUtilities.java
Original range: 474-501
Documentation:
/**
     * A utility method for drawing rotated text.
     * <P>
     * A common rotation is -Math.PI/2 which draws text 'vertically' (with the 
     * top of the characters on the left).
     *
     * @param text  the text.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param textX  the x-coordinate for the text (before rotation).
     * @param textY  the y-coordinate for the text (before rotation).
     * @param angle  the angle of the (clockwise) rotation (in radians).
     * @param rotateX  the point about which the text is rotated.
     * @param rotateY  the point about which the text is rotated.
     */
Method code:
474 |     public static void drawRotatedString(String text, Graphics2D g2,
475 |             float textX, float textY, double angle,
476 |             float rotateX, float rotateY) {
477 | 
478 |         if ((text == null) || (text.equals(""))) {
479 |             return;
480 |         }
481 | 
482 |         AffineTransform saved = g2.getTransform();
483 | 
484 |         // apply the rotation...
485 |         AffineTransform rotate = AffineTransform.getRotateInstance(
486 |                 angle, rotateX, rotateY);
487 |         g2.transform(rotate);
488 | 
489 |         if (useDrawRotatedStringWorkaround) {
490 |             // workaround for JDC bug ID 4312117 and others...
491 |             TextLayout tl = new TextLayout(text, g2.getFont(), 
492 |                     g2.getFontRenderContext());
493 |             tl.draw(g2, textX, textY);
494 |         }
495 |         else {
496 |             // replaces this code...
497 |             g2.drawString(text, textX, textY);
498 |         }
499 |         g2.setTransform(saved);
500 | 
501 |     }

148 |             else {

150 |             }
151 |         }
152 | 
153 |     }

1123 |                     - h / 2.0);

1125 |                     TextAnchor.CENTER, getLabelAngle(), TextAnchor.CENTER);

1128 |             state.cursorUp(insets.getTop() + labelBounds.getHeight() 
1129 |                     + insets.getBottom());

===== M0862 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.axis.DateAxis.autoAdjustRange()
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/axis/DateAxis.java
Original range: 1200-1251
Documentation:
/**
     * Rescales the axis to ensure that all data is visible.
     */
Method code:
1200 |     protected void autoAdjustRange() {
1201 | 
1202 |         Plot plot = getPlot();
1203 | 
1204 |         if (plot == null) {
1205 |             return;  // no plot, no data
1206 |         }
1207 | 
1208 |         if (plot instanceof ValueAxisPlot) {
1209 |             ValueAxisPlot vap = (ValueAxisPlot) plot;
1210 | 
1211 |             Range r = vap.getDataRange(this);
1212 |             if (r == null) {
1213 |                 if (this.timeline instanceof SegmentedTimeline) { 
1214 |                     //Timeline hasn't method getStartTime()
1215 |                     r = new DateRange((
1216 |                             (SegmentedTimeline) this.timeline).getStartTime(),
1217 |                             ((SegmentedTimeline) this.timeline).getStartTime() 
1218 |                             + 1);
1219 |                 } 
1220 |                 else {
1221 |                     r = new DateRange();
1222 |                 }
1223 |             }
1224 | 
1225 |             long upper = this.timeline.toTimelineValue(
1226 |                     (long) r.getUpperBound());
1227 |             long lower;
1228 |             long fixedAutoRange = (long) getFixedAutoRange();
1229 |             if (fixedAutoRange > 0.0) {
1230 |                 lower = upper - fixedAutoRange;
1231 |             }
1232 |             else {
1233 |                 lower = this.timeline.toTimelineValue((long) r.getLowerBound());
1234 |                 double range = upper - lower;
1235 |                 long minRange = (long) getAutoRangeMinimumSize();
1236 |                 if (range < minRange) {
1237 |                     long expand = (long) (minRange - range) / 2;
1238 |                     upper = upper + expand;
1239 |                     lower = lower - expand;
1240 |                 }
1241 |                 upper = upper + (long) (range * getUpperMargin());
1242 |                 lower = lower - (long) (range * getLowerMargin());
1243 |             }
1244 | 
1245 |             upper = this.timeline.toMillisecond(upper);
1246 |             lower = this.timeline.toMillisecond(lower);
1247 |             DateRange dr = new DateRange(new Date(lower), new Date(upper));
1248 |             setRange(dr, false, false);
1249 |         }
1250 | 
1251 |     }

/**
     * Creates a bar chart.  The chart object returned by this method uses a 
     * {@link CategoryPlot} instance as the plot, with a {@link CategoryAxis} 
     * for the domain axis, a {@link NumberAxis} as the range axis, and a 
     * {@link BarRenderer} as the renderer.
     *
     * @param title  the chart title (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param categoryAxisLabel  the label for the category axis 
     *                           (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param valueAxisLabel  the label for the value axis 
     *                        (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param dataset  the dataset for the chart (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * @param orientation  the plot orientation (horizontal or vertical) 
     *                     (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     * @param legend  a flag specifying whether or not a legend is required.
     * @param tooltips  configure chart to generate tool tips?
     * @param urls  configure chart to generate URLs?
     *
     * @return A bar chart.
     */
Method code:
582 |     public static JFreeChart createBarChart(String title,
583 |                                             String categoryAxisLabel,
584 |                                             String valueAxisLabel,
585 |                                             CategoryDataset dataset,
586 |                                             PlotOrientation orientation,
587 |                                             boolean legend,
588 |                                             boolean tooltips,
589 |                                             boolean urls) {
590 | 
591 |         if (orientation == null) {
592 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'orientation' argument.");
593 |         }
594 |         CategoryAxis categoryAxis = new CategoryAxis(categoryAxisLabel);
595 |         ValueAxis valueAxis = new NumberAxis(valueAxisLabel);
596 | 
597 |         BarRenderer renderer = new BarRenderer();
598 |         if (orientation == PlotOrientation.HORIZONTAL) {
599 |             ItemLabelPosition position1 = new ItemLabelPosition(
600 |                     ItemLabelAnchor.OUTSIDE3, TextAnchor.CENTER_LEFT);
601 |             renderer.setBasePositiveItemLabelPosition(position1);
602 |             ItemLabelPosition position2 = new ItemLabelPosition(
603 |                     ItemLabelAnchor.OUTSIDE9, TextAnchor.CENTER_RIGHT);
604 |             renderer.setBaseNegativeItemLabelPosition(position2);
605 |          }
606 |         else if (orientation == PlotOrientation.VERTICAL) {
607 |             ItemLabelPosition position1 = new ItemLabelPosition(
608 |                     ItemLabelAnchor.OUTSIDE12, TextAnchor.BOTTOM_CENTER);
609 |             renderer.setBasePositiveItemLabelPosition(position1);
610 |             ItemLabelPosition position2 = new ItemLabelPosition(
611 |                     ItemLabelAnchor.OUTSIDE6, TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER);
612 |             renderer.setBaseNegativeItemLabelPosition(position2);
613 |         }
614 |         if (tooltips) {
615 |             renderer.setBaseToolTipGenerator(
616 |                     new StandardCategoryToolTipGenerator());
617 |         }
618 |         if (urls) {
619 |             renderer.setBaseURLGenerator(new StandardCategoryURLGenerator());
620 |         }
621 | 
622 |         CategoryPlot plot = new CategoryPlot(dataset, categoryAxis, valueAxis, 
623 |                 renderer);
624 |         plot.setOrientation(orientation);
625 |         JFreeChart chart = new JFreeChart(title, JFreeChart.DEFAULT_TITLE_FONT,

/**

     * return <code>null</code> if {@link #isSeriesVisible(int)} or 
     * {@link #isSeriesVisibleInLegend(int)} returns <code>false</code>.

1165 |                     dataset, series);

1178 |                 urlText, shape, paint, outlineStroke, outlinePaint);

/**
     * Calculates the vertical offset between the baseline and the specified 
     * text anchor.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param anchor  the anchor.
     * 
     * @return the offset.
     */
Method code:
238 |     public float calculateBaselineOffset(Graphics2D g2, TextAnchor anchor) {
239 |         float result = 0.0f;
240 |         FontMetrics fm = g2.getFontMetrics(this.font);
241 |         LineMetrics lm = fm.getLineMetrics("ABCxyz", g2);
242 |         if (anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_LEFT || anchor == TextAnchor.TOP_CENTER

248 |                 || anchor == TextAnchor.BOTTOM_RIGHT) {
249 |             result = -lm.getDescent() - lm.getLeading();
250 |         }

Original range: 107-133
Documentation:
/**
     * Reads a <code>Paint</code> object that has been serialised by the
     * {@link SerialUtilities#writePaint(Paint, ObjectOutputStream)} method.
     *
     * @param stream  the input stream (<code>null</code> not permitted).
     *
     * @return The paint object (possibly <code>null</code>).
     *
     * @throws IOException  if there is an I/O problem.
     * @throws ClassNotFoundException  if there is a problem loading a class.
     */
Method code:
107 |     public static Paint readPaint(ObjectInputStream stream)
108 |         throws IOException, ClassNotFoundException {
109 | 
110 |         if (stream == null) {
111 |             throw new IllegalArgumentException("Null 'stream' argument.");   
112 |         }
113 |         Paint result = null;
114 |         boolean isNull = stream.readBoolean();
115 |         if (!isNull) {
116 |             Class c = (Class) stream.readObject();
117 |             if (isSerializable(c)) {
118 |                 result = (Paint) stream.readObject();
119 |             }
120 |             else if (c.equals(GradientPaint.class)) {
121 |                 float x1 = stream.readFloat();
122 |                 float y1 = stream.readFloat();
123 |                 Color c1 = (Color) stream.readObject();
124 |                 float x2 = stream.readFloat();
125 |                 float y2 = stream.readFloat();
126 |                 Color c2 = (Color) stream.readObject();
127 |                 boolean isCyclic = stream.readBoolean();
128 |                 result = new GradientPaint(x1, y1, c1, x2, y2, c2, isCyclic);
129 |             }
130 |         }
131 |         return result;
132 | 
133 |     }

177 |             return true;
178 |         }    
179 |         if (!(obj instanceof ItemLabelPosition)) {
180 |             return false;
181 |         }
182 |         ItemLabelPosition that = (ItemLabelPosition) obj;
183 |         if (!this.itemLabelAnchor.equals(that.itemLabelAnchor)) {
184 |             return false;
185 |         }
186 |         if (!this.textAnchor.equals(that.textAnchor)) {
187 |             return false;
188 |         }
189 |         if (!this.rotationAnchor.equals(that.rotationAnchor)) {
190 |             return false;
191 |         }
192 |         if (this.angle != that.angle) {
193 |             return false;
194 |         }     
195 |         return true;
196 |     }

===== M0899 =====
Signature: org.jfree.chart.block.LineBorder.draw(Graphics2D,Rectangle2D)
Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/LineBorder.java
Original range: 143-179
Documentation:
/**
     * Draws the border by filling in the reserved space (in black).
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param area  the area.
     */
Method code:
143 |     public void draw(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area) {
144 |         double w = area.getWidth();
145 |         double h = area.getHeight();
146 |         // if the area has zero height or width, we shouldn't draw anything
147 |         if (w <= 0.0 || h <= 0.0) {
148 |             return;
149 |         }
150 |         double t = this.insets.calculateTopInset(h);
151 |         double b = this.insets.calculateBottomInset(h);
152 |         double l = this.insets.calculateLeftInset(w);
153 |         double r = this.insets.calculateRightInset(w);
154 |         double x = area.getX();
155 |         double y = area.getY();
156 |         double x0 = x + l / 2.0;
157 |         double x1 = x + w - r / 2.0;
158 |         double y0 = y + h - b / 2.0;
159 |         double y1 = y + t / 2.0;
160 |         g2.setPaint(getPaint());
161 |         g2.setStroke(getStroke());
162 |         Line2D line = new Line2D.Double();
163 |         if (t > 0.0) {
164 |             line.setLine(x0, y1, x1, y1);
165 |             g2.draw(line);
166 |         }
167 |         if (b > 0.0) {
168 |             line.setLine(x0, y0, x1, y0);
169 |             g2.draw(line);
170 |         }
171 |         if (l > 0.0) {
172 |             line.setLine(x0, y0, x0, y1);
173 |             g2.draw(line);
174 |         }
175 |         if (r > 0.0) {
176 |             line.setLine(x1, y0, x1, y1);
177 |             g2.draw(line);
178 |         }        
179 |     }

/**
     * Draws the block within the specified area.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param area  the area.
     * @param params  passed on to blocks within the container 
     *                (<code>null</code> permitted).

     * @return An instance of {@link EntityBlockResult}, or <code>null</code>.

Method code:
207 |     public Object draw(Graphics2D g2, Rectangle2D area, Object params) {
208 |         // check if we need to collect chart entities from the container
209 |         EntityBlockParams ebp = null;
210 |         StandardEntityCollection sec = null;
211 |         if (params instanceof EntityBlockParams) {
212 |             ebp = (EntityBlockParams) params;
213 |             if (ebp.getGenerateEntities()) {
214 |                 sec = new StandardEntityCollection();   
215 |             }
216 |         }

218 |         contentArea = trimMargin(contentArea);
219 |         drawBorder(g2, contentArea);

222 |         Iterator iterator = this.blocks.iterator();
223 |         while (iterator.hasNext()) {
224 |             Block block = (Block) iterator.next();
225 |             Rectangle2D bounds = block.getBounds();
226 |             Rectangle2D drawArea = new Rectangle2D.Double(bounds.getX() 
227 |                     + area.getX(), bounds.getY() + area.getY(), 
228 |                     bounds.getWidth(), bounds.getHeight());
229 |             Object r = block.draw(g2, drawArea, params);
230 |             if (sec != null) {
231 |                 if (r instanceof EntityBlockResult) {
232 |                     EntityBlockResult ebr = (EntityBlockResult) r;
233 |                     EntityCollection ec = ebr.getEntityCollection();
234 |                     sec.addAll(ec);
235 |                 }
236 |             }
237 |         }
238 |         BlockResult result = null;
239 |         if (sec != null) {
240 |             result = new BlockResult();
241 |             result.setEntityCollection(sec);
242 |         }
243 |         return result;
244 |     }

/**
     * Tests this block for equality with an arbitrary object.
     * 
     * @param obj  the object (<code>null</code> permitted).
     * 
     * @return A boolean.

Method code:
557 |     public boolean equals(Object obj) {
558 |         if (obj == this) {
559 |             return true;   
560 |         }
561 |         if (!(obj instanceof AbstractBlock)) {
562 |             return false;   
563 |         }

566 |             return false;
567 |         }

569 |             return false;   
570 |         }

572 |             return false;   
573 |         }
574 |         if (!this.margin.equals(that.margin)) {
575 |             return false;   
576 |         }
577 |         if (!this.padding.equals(that.padding)) {
578 |             return false;   
579 |         }
580 |         if (this.height != that.height) {
581 |             return false;   
582 |         }
583 |         if (this.width != that.width) {
584 |             return false;   
585 |         }
586 |         return true;
587 |     }

/**
     * Draws the axis line, tick marks and tick mark labels.
     * 
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param cursor  the cursor.
     * @param plotArea  the plot area.
     * @param dataArea  the data area.

     * 
     * @return The width or height used to draw the axis.
     */
Method code:
655 |     protected AxisState drawTickMarksAndLabels(Graphics2D g2, 
656 |                                                double cursor,

659 |                                                RectangleEdge edge) {

662 | 
663 |         if (isAxisLineVisible()) {

665 |         }

692 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(cursor - ol, xx, cursor + il, xx);
693 |                 }
694 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
695 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(cursor + ol, xx, cursor - il, xx);
696 |                 }
697 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
698 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(xx, cursor - ol, xx, cursor + il);
699 |                 }
700 |                 else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
701 |                     mark = new Line2D.Double(xx, cursor + ol, xx, cursor - il);
702 |                 }
703 |                 g2.draw(mark);
704 |             }
705 |         }
706 |         
707 |         // need to work out the space used by the tick labels...
708 |         // so we can update the cursor...
709 |         double used = 0.0;
710 |         if (isTickLabelsVisible()) {
711 |             if (edge == RectangleEdge.LEFT) {
712 |                 used += findMaximumTickLabelWidth(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
713 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());  
714 |                 state.cursorLeft(used);      
715 |             }
716 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.RIGHT) {
717 |                 used = findMaximumTickLabelWidth(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
718 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
719 |                 state.cursorRight(used);      
720 |             }
721 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.TOP) {
722 |                 used = findMaximumTickLabelHeight(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
723 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
724 |                 state.cursorUp(used);
725 |             }
726 |             else if (edge == RectangleEdge.BOTTOM) {
727 |                 used = findMaximumTickLabelHeight(ticks, g2, plotArea, 
728 |                         isVerticalTickLabels());
729 |                 state.cursorDown(used);
730 |             }
731 |         }
732 |        
733 |         return state;
734 |     }

/**
     * A utility method for determining the height of a text block.
     *

     * @param position  the label position.

     *
     * @return The height.

1083 |     protected double calculateTextBlockHeight(TextBlock block, 
1084 |                                               CategoryLabelPosition position, 
1085 |                                               Graphics2D g2) {
1086 |

1092 |                 0.0f, 0.0f);

Source: source/org/jfree/chart/block/CenterArrangement.java
Original range: 94-134
Documentation:
/**
     * Calculates and sets the bounds of all the items in the specified 
     * container, subject to the given constraint.  The <code>Graphics2D</code>
     * can be used by some items (particularly items containing text) to 
     * calculate sizing parameters.
     * 
     * @param container  the container whose items are being arranged.
     * @param g2  the graphics device.
     * @param constraint  the size constraint.
     * 
     * @return The size of the container after arrangement of the contents.
     */
Method code:
94 |     public Size2D arrange(BlockContainer container, Graphics2D g2,
95 |                           RectangleConstraint constraint) {
96 |         
97 |         LengthConstraintType w = constraint.getWidthConstraintType();
98 |         LengthConstraintType h = constraint.getHeightConstraintType();
99 |         if (w == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
100 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
101 |                 return arrangeNN(container, g2);  
102 |             }
103 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
104 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
105 |             }
106 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
107 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
108 |             }
109 |         }
110 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
111 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
112 |                 return arrangeFN(container, g2, constraint);  
113 |             }
114 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
115 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
116 |             }
117 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
118 |                 throw new RuntimeException("Not implemented.");  
119 |             }
120 |         }
121 |         else if (w == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
122 |             if (h == LengthConstraintType.NONE) {
123 |                 return arrangeRN(container, g2, constraint);  
124 |             }
125 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.FIXED) {
126 |                 return arrangeRF(container, g2, constraint);  
127 |             }
128 |             else if (h == LengthConstraintType.RANGE) {
